  • 本期季节性流感病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 通过DEG分析鉴定出24个重叠DEG和13个中心基因。PPI、GO和KEGG富集分析显示这些基因主要在免疫和炎症通路中富集。scRNA-seq 分析表明,IAV 感染减少了 CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞浸润,同时增加了巨噬细胞样单核细胞的丰度,并显着下调了 IL7R 的表达,尤其是 CD4+ T 细胞中的表达。
  • 宿主 防御涉及分层的先天反应,该反应被蛋白质(例如非结构蛋白 1)拮抗,以及强大的适应性免疫,包括针对保守内部蛋白质的细胞毒性 CD8 + T 细胞和产生中和抗体的 B 细胞。 [10.1038/s41572-026-00727-5]。
  • 2025/26 季节的 A/H3N2 峰值增长率和随时间变化的繁殖数量的幅度相似,但比之前的严重季节发生得更早。 [10.1038/s44528-026-00016-3]。
  • AS03佐剂的疫苗接种在24小时内上调先天免疫细胞中的免疫途径,包括吞噬作用、抗原呈递和加工、炎性体激活、NK细胞介导的细胞毒性、IgA产生和干扰素反应途径。 [10.3389/fimmu.2026.1786231]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • 24 overlapping DEGs and 13 hub genes were identified through DEG analysis. PPI, GO, and KEGG enrichment analyses revealed these genes were mainly enriched in immune and inflammatory pathways. [10.1186/s12985-026-03272-5].
  • Host defence involves a layered innate response, which is antagonized by proteins, such as non-structural protein 1, and a robust adaptive immunity comprising cytotoxic CD8 + T cells targeting conserved internal proteins and B cells producing neutralizing antibodies.
  • Peak A/H3N2 growth rates and time-varying reproduction numbers for the 2025/26 season were of similar magnitude but occurred earlier than in previous severe seasons. [10.1038/s44528-026-00016-3].
  • AS03-adjuvanted vaccination upregulated immune pathways in innate immune cells within 24 hours, including phagocytosis, antigen presentation and processing, inflammasome activation, NK-cell mediated cytotoxicity, IgA production, and interferon-response pathways.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Communications health  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-05  |  DOI: 10.1038/s44528-026-00016-3  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 1, 1, 16

2025 年至 2026 年季节期间英格兰的 A/H3N2 流行病学 流感:数学模型研究。

Influenza A/H3N2 epidemiology in England during the 2025 to 2026 season: a mathematical modelling study.
作者： James A. Hay, Punya Alahakoon, Alexander Greenshields-Watson, Michelle Kendall, Mahan Ghafari, Chris Wymant, Robert Hinch, Luca Ferretti, Jasmina Panovska-Griffiths, Christophe Fraser
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 2025/26 年,英格兰 A/H3N2 K 亚支感染异常早期且快速增加。抗原变化和快速选择性清扫引起了人们对可能出现严重季节的担忧。基于该分支出现时进行的分析,我们的目标是将 2025/26 季节的流行病动态与前几年进行比较,并模拟合理的流行病学情景。方法 我们使用英国常规 监测 数据比较了 2011/12 至 2025/26 流感季节的峰值流行增长率和繁殖数量。使用高斯随机游走模型估计每周流行病增长率,并使用 EpiEstim 估计随时间变化的繁殖数。我们还开发了一个按年龄分层的 传播 模型和交互式网络工具,以探索不同的免疫逃逸、传播性和播种日期的场景,使用 2022/23 作为基准季节。结果 2025/26 年季节的 A/H3N2 峰值增长率和随时间变化的繁殖数量的幅度相似,但早于之前的严重季节。情景分析表明,早期趋势与中等水平的免疫逃逸、R 0 高10%或较早的播种日期相一致,尽管不可能仅从这些数据中区分这些机制的相对重要性。结论 2025/26 流感季节的特点是增长较早,但增长速度并不异常迅速。由于较早的易感性消耗加上学校假期的抑制作用,较早的增长不会系统性地导致特别大规模的流行病。抗体逃逸的实验室证据并不直接转化为群体免疫力的大幅下降,这支持了补充实时流行病学分析和建模的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
英格兰在 2025/26 季节期间 A/H3N2 流感亚支 K 感染异常早期且快速增加,引发了人们对由于抗原变化和快速选择性清除而可能出现严重流行病的担忧。
设计与对象
该研究利用英格兰常规 监测 数据将 2025/26 甲型/H3N2 流感季节的流行动态与往年进行了比较,并开发了按年龄分层的 传播 模型和交互式网络工具来探索场景。
核心方法
分别使用高斯随机游走模型和 EpiEstim 估计流行病峰值增长率和繁殖数量,同时使用 2022/23 作为基线季节进行情景分析,改变免疫逃逸、传播率和播种日期。
主要结果
2025/26 季节的 A/H3N2 峰值增长率和随时间变化的繁殖数量的幅度相似,但比之前的严重季节发生得更早。
解释与创新
2025/26年度的特点是增长较早,但并非异常快速,而且由于较早的易感枯竭和学校假期的抑制作用,较早的增长不会系统性地导致特别大的流行病。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持需要补充实时流行病学分析和建模以及抗体逃逸的实验室证据,以便为公共卫生应对措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究无法从现有数据中区分免疫逃逸、传播性增加或早期播种的相对重要性,从而限制了因果推断并强调了中等证据强度。

Influenza A/H3N2 epidemiology in England during the 2025 to 2026 season: a mathematical modelling study.

Authors: James A. Hay, Punya Alahakoon, Alexander Greenshields-Watson, Michelle Kendall, Mahan Ghafari, Chris Wymant, Robert Hinch, Luca Ferretti, Jasmina Panovska-Griffiths, Christophe Fraser
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background England experienced an unusually early and rapid increase in influenza A/H3N2 subclade K infections in 2025/26. Antigenic change and a fast selective sweep raised concerns over a potentially severe season. Building on analysis conducted as the subclade emerged, we aim to compare epidemic dynamics of the 2025/26 season to previous years and to model plausible epidemiological scenarios. Methods We compared peak epidemic growth rates and reproduction numbers across influenza seasons from 2011/12 to 2025/26 using routine surveillance data in England. Weekly epidemic growth rates were estimated using a Gaussian random walk model, and time-varying reproduction numbers using EpiEstim . We also developed an age-stratified transmission model and interactive web tool to explore scenarios varying immune escape, transmissibility, and seed date, using 2022/23 as a baseline season. Results Peak A/H3N2 growth rates and time-varying reproduction numbers for the 2025/26 season are of similar magnitude but earlier than previous severe seasons. Scenario analyses suggest early trends are compatible with moderate levels of immune escape, a 10% higher R 0 , or an earlier seed date, though it is not possible to distinguish the relative importance of these mechanisms from these data alone. Conclusions The 2025/26 influenza season is characterised by early but not unusually rapid growth. Earlier growth does not systematically lead to especially large epidemics due to earlier susceptible depletion combined with a dampening effect from school holidays. Laboratory evidence for antibody escape does not directly translate to large reductions in population immunity, supporting the need for complementary real-time epidemiological analyses and modelling.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
England experienced an unusually early and rapid increase in influenza A/H3N2 subclade K infections during the 2025/26 season, raising concerns over a potentially severe epidemic due to antigenic change and a fast selective sweep.
Design and population
The study compared epidemic dynamics of the 2025/26 influenza A/H3N2 season to previous years using routine surveillance data in England, and developed an age-stratified transmission model and interactive web tool to explore scenarios.
Core methods
Peak epidemic growth rates and reproduction numbers were estimated using a Gaussian random walk model and EpiEstim, respectively, while scenario analyses varied immune escape, transmissibility, and seed date using 2022/23 as a baseline season.
Main results
Peak A/H3N2 growth rates and time-varying reproduction numbers for the 2025/26 season were of similar magnitude but occurred earlier than in previous severe seasons.
Interpretation and novelty
The 2025/26 season is characterised by early but not unusually rapid growth, and earlier growth does not systematically lead to especially large epidemics due to earlier susceptible depletion and a dampening effect from school holidays.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the need for complementary real-time epidemiological analyses and modelling alongside laboratory evidence for antibody escape to inform public health responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study cannot distinguish the relative importance of immune escape, increased transmissibility, or earlier seeding from the available data, limiting causal inference and highlighting moderate evidence strength.

综合生物信息学分析表明,靶向激活 IL7R 可降低抑郁症患者感染甲型流感病毒的风险

An integrated bioinformatics analysis reveals that targeted activation of IL7R may reduce influenza A virus infection risk in patients with depression
作者： Zexing Chen, Yanling Xiang, Jinyi Zhu, Shixuan Li, Xinhua Wang, Changsheng Deng, Wanyi Huang
摘要中文翻译抑郁症与流感易感性增加有关;然而,其根本机制仍不清楚。进行孟德尔随机化(MR)分析来评估抑郁症和流感之间的因果关系。使用与甲型流感病毒(IAV)感染和抑郁症相关的基因表达综合(GEO)数据集进行差异表达基因(DEG)分析。采用蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用(PPI)分析、基因本体论(GO)和京都基因百科全书以及基因组(KEGG)富集分析来探索潜在机制并鉴定与抑郁症合并IAV感染相关的中枢基因。进行受试者工作特征(ROC)曲线验证和外部数据集来验证关键基因。免疫浸润和相关性分析,以及单细胞 RNA (scRNA) 测序和虚拟敲除 (KO) 模拟,用于研究改变的免疫细胞及其与关键基因的关联。通过分子对接和分子动力学模拟来筛选针对关键基因的潜在化合物。最后,使用感染 IAV 的 Jurkat T 细胞进行体外实验,进一步验证了关键基因和候选化合物的作用。MR 分析表明抑郁症对流感易感性产生有害的因果影响。通过DEG分析鉴定出24个重叠DEG和13个中心基因。PPI、GO和KEGG富集分析显示这些基因主要在免疫和炎症通路中富集。根据 ROC 曲线分析、外部验证数据集和免疫浸润分析确定,IL7R 被确定为关键基因,与多个 T 细胞亚群呈正相关,而与巨噬细胞呈负相关。scRNA-seq 分析表明,IAV 感染减少了 CD4 + 和 CD8 + T 细胞浸润,同时增加了巨噬细胞样单核细胞的丰度,并显着下调了 IL7R 的表达,特别是在 CD4 + T 细胞中。IL7R 的虚拟 KO 导致 CD4 + T 细胞中 MT-CYB 和 MT-CO1 的上调。分子对接和分子动力学模拟表明,血根碱对IL7R表现出最强的结合亲和力,并保持稳定的相互作用。体外实验进一步证实,血根碱可逆转 IAV 诱导的 IL7R 下调并减少病毒复制。IL7R可能作为潜在的治疗靶点,血根碱是通过免疫调节治疗抑郁症合并IAV感染的治疗的有希望的候选者。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索抑郁症与甲型流感病毒(IAV)感染之间的联系机制并确定潜在的治疗靶点。背景强调抑郁症与流感易感性增加有关,但其潜在机制尚不清楚。
设计与对象
使用与 IAV 感染和抑郁症相关的基因表达综合 (GEO) 数据集进行综合生物信息学分析。该研究重点关注患有抑郁症和 IAV 感染的人类受试者,分析差异表达基因 (DEG) 和免疫相关通路。
核心方法
这些方法包括差异表达基因(DEG)分析、蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用(PPI)分析、基因本体(GO)和京都基因百科全书和基因组(KEGG)富集分析、免疫浸润和相关性分析、单细胞RNA(scRNA)测序、虚拟敲除(KO)模拟、分子对接和分子动力学模拟。还进行了使用 IAV 感染的 Jurkat T 细胞的体外实验。
主要结果
通过DEG分析鉴定出24个重叠DEG和13个中心基因。PPI、GO和KEGG富集分析显示这些基因主要在免疫和炎症通路中富集。scRNA-seq 分析表明,IAV 感染减少了 CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞浸润,同时增加了巨噬细胞样单核细胞的丰度,并显着下调了 IL7R 的表达,尤其是 CD4+ T 细胞中的表达。
解释与创新
作者解释说,IL7R 可能作为一个潜在的治疗靶点,而血根碱可能是通过免疫调节治疗抑郁症合并 IAV 感染的有希望的候选药物。这是一项新颖的发现,因为它将 IL7R 表达和免疫细胞动态与抑郁症患者 IAV 感染联系起来。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对 IL7R 可以为治疗与抑郁症相关的流感易感性提供新策略,有可能改善受影响个体的免疫调节并减少病毒复制。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于生物信息学分析和体外实验,这可能会限制临床环境的普遍性。证据是温和的,因为它基于计算方法和实验室验证,没有直接的临床试验。

An integrated bioinformatics analysis reveals that targeted activation of IL7R may reduce influenza A virus infection risk in patients with depression

Authors: Zexing Chen, Yanling Xiang, Jinyi Zhu, Shixuan Li, Xinhua Wang, Changsheng Deng, Wanyi Huang
Original abstract (English)
Depression has been associated with increased susceptibility to influenza; however, the underlying mechanisms remain unclear. Mendelian randomization (MR) analysis was performed to assess the causal relationship between depression and influenza. Differential expression genes (DEGs) analyses were performed using Gene Expression Omnibus (GEO) datasets related to influenza A virus (IAV) infection and depression. Protein–protein interaction (PPI) analysis, Gene Ontology (GO) and Kyoto Encyclopedia of Genes and Genomes (KEGG) enrichment analysis were employed to explore underlying mechanisms and identify hub genes involved in depression combined with IAV infection. Receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve validation and external datasets were conducted to validate the key gene. Immune infiltration and correlation analyses, together with single-cell RNA (scRNA) sequencing and virtual knockout (KO) simulation, were used to investigate altered immune cells and their associations with the key gene. Molecular docking and molecular dynamics simulations were performed to screen potential compounds targeting the key gene. Finally, in vitro experiments using IAV-infected Jurkat T cells further validated the roles of the key gene and candidate compound. MR analysis indicated that depression exerts a detrimental causal effect on influenza susceptibility. 24 overlapping DEGs and 13 hub genes were identified through DEG analysis. PPI, GO and KEGG enrichment analyses revealed these genes were mainly enriched in immune and inflammatory pathways. IL7R was identified as a key gene and was positively correlated with multiple T-cell subsets while negatively correlated with macrophages, as determined by ROC curve analysis, external validation datasets, and immune infiltration analysis. scRNA-seq analysis demonstrated that IAV infection reduced CD4 + and CD8 + T cells infiltration while increasing macrophage-like monocytes abundance and significantly downregulating IL7R expression, particularly in CD4 + T cells. Virtual KO of IL7R led to the upregulation of MT-CYB and MT-CO1 in CD4 + T cells. Molecular docking and molecular dynamics simulations revealed that sanguinarine exhibited the strongest binding affinity for IL7R and maintained stable interaction. In vitro experiments further confirmed that sanguinarine reversed IAV-induced downregulation of IL7R and reduced viral replication. IL7R may serve as a potential therapeutic target, and sanguinarine represents a promising candidate for the treatment of depression combined with IAV infection through immune modulation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore the mechanisms linking depression and influenza A virus (IAV) infection and identify potential therapeutic targets. The background highlights that depression is associated with increased susceptibility to influenza, but the underlying mechanisms are unclear.
Design and population
An integrated bioinformatics analysis was conducted using Gene Expression Omnibus (GEO) datasets related to IAV infection and depression. The study focused on human subjects with depression and IAV infection, analyzing differential expression genes (DEGs) and immune-related pathways.
Core methods
The methods included differential expression gene (DEG) analysis, protein–protein interaction (PPI) analysis, Gene Ontology (GO) and Kyoto Encyclopedia of Genes and Genomes (KEGG) enrichment analysis, immune infiltration and correlation analyses, single-cell RNA (scRNA) sequencing, virtual knockout (KO) simulation, molecular docking, and molecular dynamics simulations. In vitro experiments using IAV-infected Jurkat T cells were also performed.
Main results
24 overlapping DEGs and 13 hub genes were identified through DEG analysis. PPI, GO, and KEGG enrichment analyses revealed these genes were mainly enriched in immune and inflammatory pathways. scRNA-seq analysis showed that IAV infection reduced CD4+ and CD8+ T cells infiltration while increasing macrophage-like monocytes abundance and significantly downregulating IL7R expression, especially in CD4+ T cells.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that IL7R may serve as a potential therapeutic target, and sanguinarine could be a promising candidate for treating depression combined with IAV infection through immune modulation. This is a novel finding as it links IL7R expression and immune cell dynamics to IAV infection in patients with depression.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeting IL7R could offer a new strategy for treating depression-related influenza susceptibility, potentially improving immune modulation and reducing viral replication in affected individuals.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on bioinformatics analysis and in vitro experiments, which may limit generalizability to clinical settings. The evidence is moderate as it is based on computational methods and laboratory validation without direct clinical trials.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

监测,2020-2024 年柬埔寨严重急性呼吸道感染患者流感和 SARS-CoV-2 阳性的趋势和决定因素:基于医院的回顾性横断面 监测 数据分析。

Epidemiology, trends and determinants of influenza and SARS-CoV-2 positivity among severe acute respiratory infection patients in Cambodia, 2020-2024: a hospital-based retrospective cross-sectional surveillance data analysis.
作者： Khemrin Pong, Hayputhik Long, Sokly Mom, Sophanith Ung, Darapheak Chau, Sengdoeurn Yi, Piseth Keam, Heng Seng, Ly Sovann
摘要中文翻译目的:描述柬埔寨住院的严重急性呼吸道感染(SARI)患者中流感和 SARS-CoV-2 的流行病学模式、时间趋势以及确诊的严重急性呼吸道感染(SARI)阳性的决定因素。设计:使用 2020 年至 2024 年收集的国家哨点 SARI 监测 数据进行基于医院的回顾性横断面 监测 数据分析。背景:9 家 SARI 哨点医院位于柬埔寨各地。参与者:共有 16 739 名符合 SARI 病例定义的住院患者。衡量标准:SARI 确诊阳性将所有实验室确诊的流感或 SARS-CoV-2 感染的 SARI 病例定义为主要结果指标。进行描述性和双变量分析,然后进行多变量逻辑回归以确定与 SARI 确认的阳性相关的因素。结果:16 739例SARI病例中,中位年龄为16岁(IQR=59),其中男性占54.6%;13.5% 经实验室确诊感染流感或 SARS-CoV-2。2021 年 死亡率 发病率较高。甲型H3N2流感病毒 在 2020 年占主导地位,随后 2021 年 SARS-CoV-2 广泛传播。2022 年,SARS-CoV-2 与 甲型H3N2流感病毒 和乙型流感(维多利亚州)共同传播。随着 SARS-CoV-2 和 甲型H3N2流感病毒 的下降,A(H1N1)pdm 成为 2023 年的主要毒株。2024年,甲型H3N2流感病毒再次引领流通,同时检测到B(维多利亚)和SARS-CoV-2。散发性 A(H5N1) 病例于 2023 年底开始出现。多变量分析表明,与 5-14 岁儿童(调整后 OR (aOR)=1.85;95% CI 1.47 至 2.31)和 50-64 岁成人(aOR=1.36;95% CI 1.15 至 1.60)相比,SARI 确诊阳性的几率更高与其他地区相比,25-49 岁成年人、雨季期间(aOR=2.09;95% CI 1.89 至 2.32)以及金边患者中(p<0.001)。继 COVID-19 大流行期间观察到流感检测下降后,2022 年至 2024 年流感检测显着增加。结论:这些结果表明,各时期 SARI 趋势有所增加,每年流行的流感亚型不断变化,并且在柬埔寨 Delta 变种 COVID-19 大流行期间 死亡率 率较高。年龄、地区、年份和季节是与 SARI 确诊阳性相关的重要因素。应优先针对特定高危人群采取有针对性的干预和预防措施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在描述 2020 年至 2024 年柬埔寨住院患者中严重急性呼吸道感染 (SARI) 确诊的流感和 SARS-CoV-2 阳性的流行病学模式、时间趋势和决定因素。
设计与对象
使用2020年至2024年在柬埔寨9家SARI哨点医院收集的国家哨点SARI 监测数据进行了基于医院的回顾性横断面监测数据分析,共涉及16,739名符合SARI病例定义的住院患者。
核心方法
进行了描述性和双变量分析,然后进行多变量逻辑回归以确定与 SARI 确诊阳性相关的因素,该阳性被定义为实验室确诊的流感或 SARS-CoV-2 感染。
主要结果
在 16,739 例 SARI 病例中,13.5% 经实验室确诊为流感或 SARS-CoV-2。甲型H3N2流感病毒 在 2020 年占主导地位,随后 SARS-CoV-2 在 2021 年广泛传播。2022 年,SARS-CoV-2 与 甲型H3N2流感病毒 和乙型流感(维多利亚州)共同传播。2023 年,随着 SARS-CoV-2 和 甲型H3N2流感病毒 的下降,A(H1N1)pdm 成为主要毒株。2024年,甲型H3N2流感病毒再次引领流通,同时检测到B(维多利亚)和SARS-CoV-2。零星 A(H5N1) 病例于 2023 年底开始出现。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了每年流行的流感亚型的变化以及 SARS-CoV-2 Delta 变体对柬埔寨 SARI 趋势的影响,其中年龄、地区、年份和季节被确定为与 SARI 确诊阳性相关的重要因素。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果深入了解了季节性流感病毒在柬埔寨的流行病学动态,强调需要在特定季节和地区对高危人群采取有针对性的干预和预防措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对 监测 数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差并影响研究结果的普遍性。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Epidemiology, trends and determinants of influenza and SARS-CoV-2 positivity among severe acute respiratory infection patients in Cambodia, 2020-2024: a hospital-based retrospective cross-sectional surveillance data analysis.

Authors: Khemrin Pong, Hayputhik Long, Sokly Mom, Sophanith Ung, Darapheak Chau, Sengdoeurn Yi, Piseth Keam, Heng Seng, Ly Sovann
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To describe the epidemiological pattern, temporal trends of influenza and SARS-CoV-2, and determinants of severe acute respiratory infection (SARI)-confirmed positivity among patients hospitalised with SARI in Cambodia. DESIGN: A hospital-based retrospective cross-sectional surveillance data analysis using national sentinel SARI surveillance data collected from 2020 to 2024. SETTING: Nine SARI sentinel hospitals are located across Cambodia. PARTICIPANTS: A total of 16 739 hospitalised patients who met the SARI case definition. MEASURE: SARI-confirmed positivity defines all SARI cases with laboratory-confirmed influenza or SARS-CoV-2 infections as the main outcome measure. Descriptive and bivariate analyses were performed, followed by multivariate logistic regression to identify factors associated with SARI-confirmed positivity. RESULTS: Among 16 739 SARI cases, median age in years was 16 (IQR=59), with 54.6% being male; 13.5% were laboratory-confirmed for influenza or SARS-CoV-2. High mortality rate in 2021. A(H3N2) dominated in 2020, followed by widespread SARS-CoV-2 circulation in 2021. In 2022, SARS-CoV-2 co-circulated with A(H3N2) and influenza B (Victoria). A(H1N1)pdm became the predominant strain in 2023 as SARS-CoV-2 and A(H3N2) declined. In 2024, A(H3N2) again led circulation, with B(Victoria) and SARS-CoV-2 also detected. Sporadic A(H5N1) cases began appearing in late 2023. Multivariable analysis indicated higher odds of SARI-confirmed positivity among children aged 5-14 years (adjusted OR (aOR)=1.85; 95% CI 1.47 to 2.31) and adults 50-64 years (aOR=1.36; 95% CI 1.15 to 1.60) compared with adults 25-49 years, during the rainy season (aOR=2.09; 95% CI 1.89 to 2.32) and among patients in Phnom Penh compared with other regions (p<0.001). Influenza detection markedly increased from 2022 to 2024 following the decline observed during the COVID-19 pandemic. CONCLUSION: These results indicate that there was an increase in SARI trends over the periods and the changing influenza subtypes circulating each year, along with a high mortality rate during the Delta variant COVID-19 pandemic in Cambodia. Age, region, year and season were significant factors associated with SARI-confirmed positivity. Targeted interventions and preventive measures should be prioritised for specific high-risk groups.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to describe the epidemiological pattern, temporal trends, and determinants of severe acute respiratory infection (SARI)-confirmed positivity for influenza and SARS-CoV-2 among hospitalized patients in Cambodia from 2020 to 2024.
Design and population
A hospital-based retrospective cross-sectional surveillance data analysis was conducted using national sentinel SARI surveillance data collected from 2020 to 2024 across nine SARI sentinel hospitals in Cambodia, involving a total of 16,739 hospitalized patients who met the SARI case definition.
Core methods
Descriptive and bivariate analyses were performed, followed by multivariate logistic regression to identify factors associated with SARI-confirmed positivity, which was defined as laboratory-confirmed influenza or SARS-CoV-2 infections.
Main results
Among 16,739 SARI cases, 13.5% were laboratory-confirmed for influenza or SARS-CoV-2. A(H3N2) dominated in 2020, followed by widespread SARS-CoV-2 circulation in 2021. In 2022, SARS-CoV-2 co-circulated with A(H3N2) and influenza B (Victoria). In 2023, A(H1N1)pdm became the predominant strain as SARS-CoV-2 and A(H3N2) declined. In 2024, A(H3N2) again led circulation, with B(Victoria) and SARS-CoV-2 also detected. Sporadic A(H5N1) cases began appearing in late 2023.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the changing influenza subtypes circulating each year and the impact of the Delta variant of SARS-CoV-2 on SARI trends in Cambodia, with age, region, year, and season identified as significant factors associated with SARI-confirmed positivity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the epidemiological dynamics of seasonal influenza viruses in Cambodia, emphasizing the need for targeted interventions and preventive measures for high-risk groups during specific seasons and regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on surveillance data, which may introduce bias and affect the generalisability of findings. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

队列研究 研究对象为 2023 年至 2025 年在英格兰有受感染动物的场所接触高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 的人员

A cohort study of persons exposed to highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) at premises with infected animals, England, 2023 to 2025
作者： Florence Halford, Paula Blomquist, Neil Bray, Chloe Byers, Carmellie Inzoungou-Massanga, Carys Rees, Maria Waghorn, Motolani Awokoya, Rachel Lunt, Neil Cunningham
摘要中文翻译背景持续存在的高致病性甲型禽流感(H5N1)的流行对人类健康构成风险,既来自接触受感染鸟类或哺乳动物引起的感染,也来自潜在的 传播 病毒,而这种病毒几乎没有或没有群体免疫力。目的本 队列研究 研究的目的是为评估禽流感对人类健康的风险和流感的公共卫生管理提供信息。A(H5N1) 暴露。方法我们于 2023 年 4 月至 2025 年 3 月期间在英格兰各地的 34 个高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 暴发 地点招募了 428 名个体。通过鼻咽样本和问卷调查,我们调查了危险因素,包括暴露时间和个人防护装备(PPE)的使用情况,以及甲型H5N1流感感染的特征。结果参与者年龄中位数为37岁(四分位距:28-51岁),296名(69%)为男性。大多数参与者 (85%) 报告在暴露时充分使用了个人防护装备,82 名参与者 (19%) 接种了 季节性流感 疫苗。6 人检测结果呈甲型 H5N1 流感 PCR 阳性,其中 3 人(< 1%)因暴露时间而符合感染病例定义(2 人确诊,1 人不明)。没有报告严重疾病;没有发现继发病例。6例检测呈阳性的病例均未接种季节性流感疫苗;其中两人报告在暴露时没有穿戴全套个人防护装备。结论我们建议在检测到动物与人类 传播 的风险增加后,继续认真使用个人防护装备,并恢复增强型 监测,以“同一个健康”为重点,以降低甲型流感 (H5N1) 的大流行风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
持续存在的高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 流行对人类健康构成威胁,一方面是由于接触受感染的鸟类或哺乳动物而造成的感染,另一方面是由于这种病毒可能在人与人之间进行 传播,而这种病毒几乎没有或没有群体免疫力。
设计与对象
这项 队列研究 研究旨在为评估禽流感对人类健康的风险以及甲型流感 (H5N1) 暴露的公共卫生管理提供信息。2023 年 4 月至 2025 年 3 月期间,它在英格兰的 34 个 暴发 站点招募了 428 名人员。
核心方法
通过鼻咽样本和问卷调查,我们调查了暴露时间、个人防护装备(PPE)使用情况等危险因素,以及甲型H5N1流感感染特征。
主要结果
6 人检测结果呈甲型 H5N1 流感 PCR 阳性,其中 3 人 (< 1%) 符合因暴露时间而感染的病例定义。没有报告严重疾病;没有发现继发病例。6例检测呈阳性的病例均未接种季节性流感疫苗;其中两人报告称,暴露时没有穿戴全套个人防护装备。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明继续认真使用个人防护装备和加强 监测 对于减轻甲型流感 (H5N1) 大流行风险是必要的。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要继续使用个人防护装备并加强 监测,以监测和减轻动物与人类之间感染 A(H5N1) 流感的 传播 风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于自我报告的个人防护装备使用情况以及 PCR 阳性病例数量较少,这可能会影响证据的强度。证据的强度中等。

A cohort study of persons exposed to highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) at premises with infected animals, England, 2023 to 2025

Authors: Florence Halford, Paula Blomquist, Neil Bray, Chloe Byers, Carmellie Inzoungou-Massanga, Carys Rees, Maria Waghorn, Motolani Awokoya, Rachel Lunt, Neil Cunningham
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUNDThe ongoing panzootic of highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) presents a risk to human health both from infections resulting from exposure to infected birds or mammals, and from potential mutations enabling human-to-human transmission of a virus to which there is little or no population immunity.AIMThis cohort study was designed with the aim of informing assessments of the risk of avian influenza to human health and the public health management of influenza A(H5N1) exposures.METHODSWe recruited 428 individuals at 34 highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) outbreak sites between April 2023 to March 2025, throughout England. Through nasopharyngeal samples and questionnaires, we investigated risk factors including exposure periods and usage of personal protective equipment (PPE), and characteristics of influenza A(H5N1) infection.RESULTSThe median participant age was 37 years (interquartile range: 28-51 years), 296 (69%) were male. Most participants (85%) reported full PPE use when exposed, and 82 (19%) were vaccinated against seasonal influenza. Six persons tested PCR-positive for influenza A(H5N1), of whom three (< 1%) met the case definition for infection (two confirmed, one unclear) attributable to exposure periods. No severe illness was reported; no secondary cases were identified. None of the six cases with positive detections were vaccinated against seasonal influenza; two of them reported not wearing full PPE when exposed.CONCLUSIONWe recommend continued conscientious PPE use and the resumption of enhanced surveillance following detection of an increased risk of animal-human transmission, with a One Health focus, to mitigate pandemic risk of influenza A(H5N1).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The ongoing panzootic of highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) presents a risk to human health both from infections resulting from exposure to infected birds or mammals, and from potential mutations enabling human-to-human transmission of a virus to which there is little or no population immunity.
Design and population
This cohort study was designed to inform assessments of the risk of avian influenza to human health and the public health management of influenza A(H5N1) exposures. It recruited 428 individuals at 34 outbreak sites in England between April 2023 and March 2025.
Core methods
Through nasopharyngeal samples and questionnaires, we investigated risk factors including exposure periods and usage of personal protective equipment (PPE), and characteristics of influenza A(H5N1) infection.
Main results
Six persons tested PCR-positive for influenza A(H5N1), of whom three (< 1%) met the case definition for infection attributable to exposure periods. No severe illness was reported; no secondary cases were identified. None of the six cases with positive detections were vaccinated against seasonal influenza; two of them reported not wearing full PPE when exposed.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that continued conscientious PPE use and enhanced surveillance are necessary to mitigate pandemic risk of influenza A(H5N1).
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for continued PPE use and enhanced surveillance to monitor and mitigate the risk of animal-human transmission of influenza A(H5N1).
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported PPE use and the small number of PCR-positive cases, which may affect the strength of the evidence. The evidence is moderate in strength.

随机细胞内复制动态塑造甲型流感病毒群体水平的 进化

Stochastic intracellular replication dynamics shape population-level evolution in Influenza A Virus
作者： Ernesto Alejandro Segredo-Otero, David Gresham
摘要中文翻译摘要 了解甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 感染的遗传多样性来源对于了解病毒 进化 和免疫逃逸的机制至关重要。尽管先前的研究已经描述了 宿主-to-宿主 传播 和宿主内组织间传播过程中群体瓶颈的影响,但细胞内复制过程对 IAV 遗传多样性的作用仍然很大程度上未被探索。在这项研究中,我们使用随机数学模型来模拟遗传上不同的 IAV 毒株在单个细胞和组织内的复制。我们的结果揭示了单个细胞的单个感染周期内的显着瓶颈效应。细胞内瓶颈效应由随机分子过程驱动,导致中性变异的扩展或消除,从而在单个细胞中遗传变异的初始频率和最终频率之间产生大规模差异。通过将我们的研究结果扩展到群体水平模型,我们表明IAV细胞内复制减少了有效群体规模,从而减少了选择的影响并增加了遗传漂变的作用。我们的研究结果强调了细胞内复制过程对 IAV 遗传多样性的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
了解甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 感染的遗传多样性来源对于了解病毒 进化 和免疫逃逸的机制至关重要。
设计与对象
先前的研究已经描述了 宿主 到 宿主 传播 和宿主内组织间传播过程中群体瓶颈的影响,但细胞内复制过程对 IAV 遗传多样性的作用在很大程度上仍未被探索。
核心方法
在这项研究中,随机数学模型被用来模拟遗传上不同的 IAV 毒株在单个细胞和组织内的复制。
主要结果
我们的结果揭示了单个细胞的单个感染周期内的显着瓶颈效应。
解释与创新
细胞内瓶颈效应由随机分子过程驱动,导致中性变异的扩展或消除,从而在单个细胞中遗传变异的初始频率和最终频率之间产生大规模差异。
科研与公卫意义
通过将我们的研究结果扩展到群体水平模型,我们表明IAV细胞内复制减少了有效群体规模,从而减少了选择的影响并增加了遗传漂变的作用。
局限与证据强度
我们的研究结果强调了细胞内复制过程对 IAV 遗传多样性的影响,但所提供的证据没有报告具体的局限性或证据强度评估。

Stochastic intracellular replication dynamics shape population-level evolution in Influenza A Virus

Authors: Ernesto Alejandro Segredo-Otero, David Gresham
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Understanding the sources of genetic diversity in Influenza A Virus (IAV) infections is crucial for understanding the mechanisms of viral evolution and immune escape. Whereas prior studies have characterized the effects of population bottlenecks during host-to-host transmission and intrahost tissue-to-tissue dissemination, the role of intracellular replication processes on IAV genetic diversity remains largely unexplored. In this study, we used stochastic mathematical modeling to simulate the replication of genetically distinct IAV strains within individual cells and tissues. Our results reveal significant bottleneck effects within a single infection cycle of individual cells. Intracellular bottleneck effects are driven by stochastic molecular processes and lead to the expansion or elimination of neutral variants creating large-scale differences between the initial and final frequencies of genetic variants in individual cells. By expanding our findings to a population-level model, we show that IAV intracellular replication reduces the effective population size, thereby diminishing the impact of selection and increasing the role of genetic drift. Our findings highlight the impact of intracellular replication processes on IAV genetic diversity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Understanding the sources of genetic diversity in Influenza A Virus (IAV) infections is crucial for understanding the mechanisms of viral evolution and immune escape.
Design and population
Prior studies have characterized the effects of population bottlenecks during host-to-host transmission and intrahost tissue-to-tissue dissemination, but the role of intracellular replication processes on IAV genetic diversity remains largely unexplored.
Core methods
In this study, stochastic mathematical modeling was used to simulate the replication of genetically distinct IAV strains within individual cells and tissues.
Main results
Our results reveal significant bottleneck effects within a single infection cycle of individual cells.
Interpretation and novelty
Intracellular bottleneck effects are driven by stochastic molecular processes and lead to the expansion or elimination of neutral variants, creating large-scale differences between the initial and final frequencies of genetic variants in individual cells.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
Our findings highlight the impact of intracellular replication processes on IAV genetic diversity, but the supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessment.

AS03 佐剂的甲型流感/H7N9 疫苗 后的转录组学和蛋白质组学特征

Transcriptomic and proteomic signatures following AS03-adjuvanted Influenza A/H7N9 vaccine
作者： C. Buddy Creech, Kristen J. Steenbergen, Casey E. Gelber, Leigh M. Howard, Kevin L. Schey, Kristie Lindsey Rose, Sandra M. Yoder, Kathryn M. Edwards, Johannes B. Goll
摘要中文翻译简介 疫苗 针对禽流感病毒 A/H7N9 的免疫原性较差。虽然 免疫应答 可以通过水包油乳剂佐剂(例如佐剂系统 03 (AS03))得到改善,但支撑佐剂作用的细胞机制尚未完全表征且知之甚少。方法 我们招募了 30 名健康成人参与者,并使用 RNA 测序和定量蛋白质组学来表征六种免疫细胞类型对两种剂量的甲型/H7N9 流感 疫苗(含和不含 AS03)的反应。将这些反应与施用未加佐剂的季节性流感 A/H3N2 变体 疫苗 后观察到的反应进行比较,以鉴定加佐剂的流感 疫苗 特有的特征,并与后来的抗体反应相关。转录组和蛋白质组分析揭示了这一点。结果 AS03 佐剂的 疫苗 与 疫苗接种 后 24 小时内先天免疫细胞中的免疫途径上调相关,包括吞噬作用、抗原呈递和加工、炎性体激活、NK 细胞介导的细胞毒性、IgA 产生和干扰素反应途径。此外,虽然在多种免疫细胞类型中观察到主要组织相容性复合体(MHC I 和 II)上调,但中性粒细胞区室中的 MHCII 基因转录也增加,从而产生了中性粒细胞在抗原呈递中发挥比以前理解的更重要作用的假设。讨论 综上所述,这些数据提供了对水包油佐剂及其在增强 免疫应答 大流行性流感防范方面的作用的更完整的机制理解。临床试验注册 https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT02921997?term=NCT02921997&viewType,标识符 NCT02921997。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
禽源甲型/H7N9流感疫苗在人体中的免疫原性很差,并且水包油乳剂佐剂(例如AS03)增强免疫应答的细胞机制仍未完全表征。
设计与对象
该研究招募了 30 名健康成年人,并使用 RNA 测序和定量蛋白质组学来分析六种免疫细胞类型对两剂 AS03 佐剂的 A/H7N9 疫苗 的反应,并将这些反应与未佐剂的季节性 A/H3N2 疫苗 引起的反应进行比较。
核心方法
进行转录组学和蛋白质组学分析,以鉴定 AS03 佐剂的 疫苗 独特的免疫特征,并将这些特征与随后的抗体反应相关联。
主要结果
AS03佐剂的疫苗接种在24小时内上调先天免疫细胞中的免疫途径,包括吞噬作用、抗原呈递和加工、炎性体激活、NK细胞介导的细胞毒性、IgA产生和干扰素反应途径。
解释与创新
这些发现为 AS03 如何增强 疫苗 免疫原性提供了一个机制框架,强调了中性粒细胞在 MHCII 介导的抗原呈递中的作用,作为佐剂 疫苗 反应中先前未被充分认识的组成部分。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果通过识别早期转录组和蛋白质组特征为大流行性流感做好准备提供信息,这些特征可以指导针对新出现的流感病毒株的佐剂 疫苗 的开发和评估。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小和缺乏无佐剂的 A/H7N9 比较臂的限制,这限制了普遍性和因果推理的强度;证据被评估为中等。

Transcriptomic and proteomic signatures following AS03-adjuvanted Influenza A/H7N9 vaccine

Authors: C. Buddy Creech, Kristen J. Steenbergen, Casey E. Gelber, Leigh M. Howard, Kevin L. Schey, Kristie Lindsey Rose, Sandra M. Yoder, Kathryn M. Edwards, Johannes B. Goll
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Vaccines targeting avian influenza virus A/H7N9 are poorly immunogenic. While the immune responses can be improved with oil-in-water emulsion adjuvants such as Adjuvant System 03 (AS03), the cellular mechanisms underpinning the adjuvant effect are incompletely characterized and poorly understood. Methods We enrolled 30 healthy adult participants and used RNA sequencing and quantitative proteomics to characterize the response to two doses of the influenza A/H7N9 vaccine, with and without AS03, in six immune cell types. These responses were compared to those seen after administration of an unadjuvanted seasonal in uenza A/H3N2 variant vaccine to identify signatures unique to adjuvanted influenza vaccines and correlated with later antibody responses. Transcriptomic and proteomic analyses revealed that. Results AS03-adjuvanted vaccine was associated with upregulation of immune pathways in innate immune cells within 24h following vaccination for phagocytosis, antigen presentation and processing, inflammasome activation, NK-cell mediated cytotoxicity, IgA production, and interferon-response pathways. Moreover, while major histocompatibility complex (MHC I and II) upregulation was observed across multiple immune cell types, MHCII gene transcription was also increased in the neutrophil compartment, generating the hypothesis that neutrophils may play a more important role in antigen presentation than previously understood. Discussion Taken together, these data provide a more complete mechanistic understanding of oil-in-water adjuvants and their role in enhancing the immune response for pandemic influenza preparedness. Clinical Trial Registration https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT02921997?term=NCT02921997&viewType , idientifier NCT02921997.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Avian-origin influenza A/H7N9 vaccines are poorly immunogenic in humans, and the cellular mechanisms by which oil-in-water emulsion adjuvants such as AS03 enhance immune responses remain incompletely characterized.
Design and population
The study enrolled 30 healthy adults and used RNA sequencing and quantitative proteomics to profile responses to two doses of an AS03-adjuvanted A/H7N9 vaccine across six immune cell types, comparing these responses to those elicited by an unadjuvanted seasonal A/H3N2 vaccine.
Core methods
Transcriptomic and proteomic profiling were performed to identify immune signatures unique to the AS03-adjuvanted vaccine and to correlate these signatures with subsequent antibody responses.
Main results
AS03-adjuvanted vaccination upregulated immune pathways in innate immune cells within 24 hours, including phagocytosis, antigen presentation and processing, inflammasome activation, NK-cell mediated cytotoxicity, IgA production, and interferon-response pathways.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide a mechanistic framework for how AS03 enhances vaccine immunogenicity, highlighting the role of neutrophils in MHCII-mediated antigen presentation as a previously underappreciated component of adjuvanted vaccine responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results inform pandemic influenza preparedness by identifying early transcriptomic and proteomic signatures that may guide the development and evaluation of adjuvanted vaccines against emerging influenza strains.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and the absence of a non-adjuvanted A/H7N9 comparator arm, which constrains generalizability and the strength of causal inference; the evidence is assessed as moderate.

确定 B/山形流感全球明显灭绝背后的病毒和流行病学因素

Identifying the viral and epidemiological factors behind the apparent global extinction of influenza B/Yamagata
作者： Gutierrez, M. A., Rohani, P. A.
摘要中文翻译直到 2020 年,两种乙型流感病毒谱系在全球范围内共同传播。COVID-19 大流行导致几乎没有流感感染。虽然 B/Victoria 于 2021 年底重新出现,但自大流行以来尚未有 B/Yamagata 的报告。为了调查哪些流行病学和免疫学因素是导致 B/Yamagata 灭绝的主要原因,我们开发了两个 B 型流感谱系的全球模型。为了模拟大流行的 传播 影响,我们暂时减少了接触,并确定了参数值,这些参数值概括了大流行之前的病毒共存动态以及 B/Victoria(于 2021 年底重新出现)和 B/Yamagata(灭绝)的大流行后定性结果。我们的结果表明,B/Yamagata 的灭绝主要是由于其较低的基本繁殖数,而不是免疫学或进化机制,使得该病毒在大流行的早期阶段特别脆弱。我们的最佳拟合模型的随机模拟表明,B/Victoria 在此期间也接近灭绝。我们研究了该模型,以评估通过 疫苗接种 根除 B/Victoria 的可行性以及 B/Yamagata 在 2026-27 流感季节持续重新出现的可能性,从而强调生物安全的重要考虑因素。意义 几十年来,乙型流感病毒的山形和维多利亚谱系在全球范围内共同传播,导致数百万人感染。在 COVID-19 大流行之后,只有维多利亚重新出现。通过将机制 传播 模型与全球流感 监测 数据相结合,我们发现山形灭绝与幸存的维多利亚谱系相比较低的内在传播率最为一致。与其他提出的解释相反,交叉免疫和免疫逃逸的差异似乎不足以解释观察到的结果。我们的分析进一步表明,维多利亚在 COVID-19 破坏期间勉强避免了灭绝,但现在如果重新引入该谱系,山形的 传播 将得以维持。这些结果为根除流感工作和生物安全政策提供了信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解释了为什么 B/Yamagata 谱系在 COVID-19 大流行后似乎在全球范围内灭绝,而 B/Victoria 谱系却重新出现。它试图确定流行病学因素还是免疫学因素是否是 B/Yamagata 灭绝的主要原因。
设计与对象
作者为两种 B 型流感谱系(B/Victoria 和 B/Yamagata)开发了一个全局机制 传播 模型,以模拟它们在 COVID-19 大流行之前和之后的动态。
核心方法
构建了一个全球模型来模拟乙型流感 传播 动态,其中包括接触量的暂时减少以模拟大流行的影响。确定参数值以概括两个谱系在大流行前的共存和大流行后的结果。
主要结果
该模型表明,B/Yamagata 的灭绝主要是由于其较低的基本繁殖数,使其在大流行早期阶段特别脆弱。随机模拟表明 B/Victoria 在此期间也濒临灭绝。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,内在传播性的差异,而不是交叉免疫或免疫逃逸,最能解释 B/Yamagata 的灭绝。他们强调,他们的分析独特地将机械模型与全球 监测 数据相结合,以测试相互竞争的假设。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,B/Yamagata 的灭绝可能是由于其较低的传播性,同时还表明 B/Victoria 勉强避免了灭绝,并且如果重新引入,可以维持 传播,从而为流感根除策略和生物安全政策提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于建模框架,不包括经验免疫学或进化数据,这限制了机制的直接确认。由于缺乏实验验证且依赖 监测 数据,证据强度中等。

Identifying the viral and epidemiological factors behind the apparent global extinction of influenza B/Yamagata

Authors: Gutierrez, M. A., Rohani, P. A.
Original abstract (English)
Until 2020, two lineages of the influenza B virus had co-circulated globally. The COVID-19 pandemic led to a near-absence of influenza infections. While B/Victoria reemerged in late 2021, there have been no reports of B/Yamagata since the pandemic. To investigate which epidemiological and immunological factors were primarily responsible for the extinction of B/Yamagata, we developed a global model for the two influenza B lineages. To mimic the transmission impacts of the pandemic, we implemented a transient reduction in contacts and identified parameter values that recapitulated viral coexistence dynamic before the pandemic and the qualitative post-pandemic outcomes of B/Victoria (reemergence in late 2021) and B/Yamagata (extinction). Our results suggest that, rather than immunological or evolutionary mechanisms, the extinction of B/Yamagata was mainly driven by its lower basic reproduction number making the virus particularly vulnerable during the early phase of the pandemic. Stochastic simulations of our best-fitting model suggest that B/Victoria was also close to extinction during this period. We investigate the model to assess the feasibility of B/Victoria eradication through vaccination and the potential for a sustained re-emergence of B/Yamagata in the 2026-27 flu season, thus highlighting important considerations for biosafety. SignificanceFor decades, the Yamagata and Victoria lineages of the influenza B virus co-circulated globally, causing millions of infections in humans. Only Victoria reemerged after the COVID-19 pandemic. By combining mechanistic transmission modeling with worldwide influenza surveillance data, we show that Yamagatas extinction is most consistent with a lower intrinsic transmissibility than that of the surviving Victoria lineage. Contrary to other proposed explanations, differences in cross-immunity and immune escape appear insufficient to explain the observed outcome. Our analyses further indicate that Victoria narrowly avoided extinction during the COVID-19 disruption, but now transmission of Yamagata would be sustained if the lineage were reintroduced. These results inform both influenza eradication efforts and biosafety policy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed why the influenza B/Yamagata lineage appeared to go globally extinct after the COVID-19 pandemic while the B/Victoria lineage reemerged. It sought to determine whether epidemiological or immunological factors were primarily responsible for the extinction of B/Yamagata.
Design and population
The authors developed a global mechanistic transmission model for the two influenza B lineages (B/Victoria and B/Yamagata) to simulate their dynamics before and after the COVID-19 pandemic.
Core methods
A global model was constructed to simulate influenza B transmission dynamics, incorporating a transient reduction in contacts to mimic pandemic impacts. Parameter values were identified to recapitulate pre-pandemic coexistence and post-pandemic outcomes for both lineages.
Main results
The model indicated that B/Yamagata’s extinction was primarily driven by its lower basic reproduction number, making it particularly vulnerable during the early pandemic phase. Stochastic simulations suggested B/Victoria was also close to extinction during this period.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that differences in intrinsic transmissibility, rather than cross-immunity or immune escape, best explained B/Yamagata’s extinction. They highlighted that their analysis uniquely combined mechanistic modeling with worldwide surveillance data to test competing hypotheses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform influenza eradication strategies and biosafety policies by demonstrating that B/Yamagata’s extinction was likely due to its lower transmissibility, while also showing that B/Victoria narrowly avoided extinction and could sustain transmission if reintroduced.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on a modeling framework and does not include empirical immunological or evolutionary data, which limits direct confirmation of mechanisms. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of experimental validation and reliance on surveillance data.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

2024-25 年流感季节 抗病毒药物 抗性病毒的检测和表征。

Detection and characterization of antiviral-resistant viruses during the influenza season of 2024-25.
作者： Mira C. Patel, Ha T. Nguyen, Philippe Noriel Q. Pascua, Mercedes Lopez-Esteva, Chloe Champion, Vasiliy P. Mishin, Han Di, Kristine A. Lacek, Marie K. Kirby, Allen Bateman
摘要中文翻译未标记:在 2024-25 年的高危季节,CDC 与公共卫生合作伙伴对超过 10,000 种流感病毒的 基因组 进行了测序和分析,以寻找 基因组 抗性标记。使用 抗病毒药物 使用体外测定对可用的序列标记病毒和代表性病毒进行了测试。在美国,三种奥司他韦耐药 甲型H3N2流感病毒 病毒具有 治疗 突现神经氨酸酶 (NA) 突变,E119V 或 R292K。在 15 个州检测到带有 NA-H275Y 的奥司他韦耐药 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒,尽管频率较低(0.53%)。它们属于多个系统发育群体,其中最常见的是血凝素 (HA) 亚支 D.3.1 与 NA 亚支 D.1 或 D.2 组合。根据共享序列数据,几乎所有来自澳大利亚、加拿大和智利的 H275Y 病毒也属于这些 HA 和 NA 亚支。相反,来自中国的大多数 H275Y 病毒 (68/81) 属于 HA 亚型 C.1.9 和 NA 亚型 D,并共享许可型 突变 R257K。在 9 种甲型流感病毒中检测到流感聚合酶酸性 (PA) 突变 使巴洛沙韦敏感性降低 4 至 92 倍。带有 PA-I38T 的病毒在三种细胞系中表现出复制适应性的轻微减弱。根据现有数据,从未接触过 抗病毒药物 的患者中收集了 NA-H275Y 和 PA-I38T 病毒。与前几季相比,美国批准的所有流感 抗病毒药物 的基线易感性基本保持不变。在美国检测到的所有猪源病毒均具有金刚烷抗性标记 M2-S31N,但仍然对其他批准的 抗病毒药物 敏感。随着公共卫生实验室测序能力和生物信息支持的增加,抗病毒药物 易感性监测得到了显着改善。通过流感 监测 获得的信息已用于指导 抗病毒药物 使用建议。重要性:对 抗病毒药物 敏感性降低的流感病毒的循环可能会降低流感药物的有效性。这项研究提供了 2024-25 年流感高发季美国耐药流感病毒 患病率 的信息。它提供了有关所有已批准的 抗病毒药物 药物的敏感性概况以及代表性耐药病毒的复制适应性的信息。大多数耐药病毒是从未接触过 抗病毒药物 的患者中收集的,表明它们具有人际传播能力。基于全 基因组 序列 (WGS) 的分析是 监测 的基石,许多实验室一直在使用这种方法。然而,疾病预防控制中心实验室是美国唯一一家对流行病毒进行表型测试的实验室,以确认基于序列的分析结果并确定新的耐药分子标记。通过病毒学 监测 收集的数据提供了急需的有关流感病毒药物敏感性的信息,这些信息可用于指导 抗病毒药物 使用的建议。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了公共卫生需要监测 季节性流感 病毒,以降低在 2024-25 年严重程度期间对 抗病毒药物 药物的敏感性,此时耐药变异的传播可能会破坏 治疗 的有效性。其目的是量化 抗病毒药物 耐药标记并评估其对药物敏感性和病毒适应性的影响。
设计与对象
CDC 和公共卫生合作伙伴对 2024-25 年美国流感季节期间收集的 10,000 多种流感病毒进行了 基因组 测序和分析。在体外测试了序列标记病毒和代表性病毒的 抗病毒药物 敏感性。
核心方法
全 基因组 测序鉴定了 抗病毒药物 抗性标记,表型分析测量了循环病毒的药物敏感性。疾病预防控制中心的实验室进行了表型测试,以确认基于序列的预测并确定新的耐药分子标记。
主要结果
在 15 个州以 0.53% 的低频率检测到带有 NA-H275Y 的奥司他韦耐药 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09 病毒。在 9 种甲型流感病毒中检测到流感聚合酶酸性 (PA) 突变 使巴洛沙韦敏感性降低 4 至 92 倍,PA-I38T 在三种细胞系中显示复制适应性轻度减弱。
解释与创新
作者将 NA-H275Y 和 PA-I38T 病毒的低但持续的循环解释为人与人之间 传播 的证据,尽管大多数患者没有报告 传播 暴露。他们强调了 基因组 测序对 监测 的实用性以及表型确认以验证基于序列的抗性预测的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现通过记录 患病率 和耐药 季节性流感 病毒的适应性、指导 抗病毒药物 使用建议并支持扩大公共卫生实验室中基于测序的 监测 能力,为 抗病毒药物 管理提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的优势得益于大规模测序和表型确认,但对 CDC 实验室进行表型测试的依赖限制了通量和普遍性。由于缺乏详细的临床暴露数据以及哨点之外的耐药性可能未检测到,因此证据是中等的。

Detection and characterization of antiviral-resistant viruses during the influenza season of 2024-25.

Authors: Mira C. Patel, Ha T. Nguyen, Philippe Noriel Q. Pascua, Mercedes Lopez-Esteva, Chloe Champion, Vasiliy P. Mishin, Han Di, Kristine A. Lacek, Marie K. Kirby, Allen Bateman
Original abstract (English)
UNLABELLED: During the high severity season of 2024-25, CDC with public health partners sequenced and analyzed genomes of >10,000 influenza viruses for antiviral resistance markers. Available sequence-flagged and representative viruses were tested with antivirals using in vitro assays. In the US, three oseltamivir-resistant A(H3N2) viruses had treatment-emergent neuraminidase (NA) mutations, either E119V or R292K. Oseltamivir-resistant A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses with NA-H275Y were detected in 15 states, albeit at a low frequency (0.53%). They belonged to several phylogenetic groups, with hemagglutinin (HA) subclade D.3.1 combined with either NA subclade D.1 or D.2 being most common. Based on shared sequence data, nearly all H275Y viruses from Australia, Canada, and Chile also belonged to these HA and NA subclades. Conversely, most H275Y viruses (68/81) from China belonged to HA subclade C.1.9 and NA subclade D and shared the permissive mutation R257K. Influenza polymerase acidic (PA) mutations conferring 4- to 92-fold decreased baloxavir susceptibility were detected in nine influenza A viruses. Viruses with PA-I38T showed mild attenuation of replicative fitness in three cell lines. Based on available data, NA-H275Y and PA-I38T viruses were collected from patients with no exposure to antivirals. Baseline susceptibility to all US-approved influenza antivirals remained largely unchanged compared to previous seasons. All swine-origin viruses detected in the US had adamantane resistance-conferring marker, M2-S31N, but remained susceptible to other approved antivirals. Monitoring antiviral susceptibility has substantially improved with increased sequencing capacities and bioinformatic support at public health laboratories. Information gained through influenza surveillance has been used to guide recommendations on antiviral use. IMPORTANCE: Circulation of influenza viruses with reduced susceptibility to antivirals can diminish the usefulness of medications prescribed for influenza. This study informs on the prevalence of drug-resistant influenza viruses in the US during the high severity season of 2024-25. It provides information on susceptibility profile to all approved antiviral medications and on replicative fitness of representative drug-resistant viruses. Most drug-resistant viruses were collected from patients who were not exposed to antivirals indicating their ability to transmit from human to human. Whole-genome sequence (WGS)-based analysis is the cornerstone for surveillance, and numerous laboratories have been utilizing this approach. However, CDC laboratory is the only laboratory in the US conducting phenotypic testing of circulating viruses needed to confirm the outcomes of sequence-based analysis and to identify new molecular markers of resistance. Data gathered through virologic surveillance give much-needed information on drug susceptibility of influenza viruses which are used to guide recommendations on antiviral use.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health need to monitor seasonal influenza viruses for reduced susceptibility to antiviral drugs during the high-severity 2024–25 season, when circulation of resistant variants could undermine treatment effectiveness. It aimed to quantify antiviral resistance markers and assess their impact on drug susceptibility and viral fitness.
Design and population
CDC and public-health partners sequenced and analyzed genomes of more than 10,000 influenza viruses collected during the 2024–25 US influenza season. Sequence-flagged and representative viruses were tested in vitro for antiviral susceptibility.
Core methods
Whole-genome sequencing identified antiviral resistance markers, and phenotypic assays measured drug susceptibility of circulating viruses. CDC’s laboratory performed phenotypic testing to confirm sequence-based predictions and to identify new molecular markers of resistance.
Main results
Oseltamivir-resistant A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses with NA-H275Y were detected in 15 states at a low frequency of 0.53%. Influenza polymerase acidic (PA) mutations conferring 4- to 92-fold decreased baloxavir susceptibility were detected in nine influenza A viruses, and PA-I38T showed mild attenuation of replicative fitness in three cell lines.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the low but persistent circulation of NA-H275Y and PA-I38T viruses as evidence of human-to-human transmission despite no reported antiviral exposure in most patients. They highlighted the utility of whole-genome sequencing for surveillance and the necessity of phenotypic confirmation to validate sequence-based resistance predictions.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform antiviral stewardship by documenting the prevalence and fitness of drug-resistant seasonal influenza viruses, guiding recommendations on antiviral use and supporting the expansion of sequencing-based surveillance capacity in public-health laboratories.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s strength is bolstered by large-scale sequencing and phenotypic confirmation, but reliance on CDC laboratory for phenotypic testing limits throughput and generalizability. The evidence is moderate due to the absence of detailed clinical exposure data and potential under-detection of resistance outside sentinel sites.

H3N2 流感病毒血凝素蛋白上 B 细胞表位的鉴定和计算分析。

Identification and computational analysis of B-cell epitopes on the hemagglutinin protein of the H3N2 influenza virus.
作者： Mengyuan Guo, Xue Wang, Jingming Zhou, Yumei Chen, Chao Liang, Haili Wang, Sixuan Wu, Xifang Zhu, Aiping Wang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲型流感病毒会引起季节性呼吸道疾病,其血凝素 (HA) 蛋白会发生持续的抗原变异,从而引发大流行。这项研究是必要的,因为精确鉴定 HA 内的 B 细胞表位对于开发诊断工具和 疫苗 候选药物以限制流感传播至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究采用综合实验和生物信息学策略设计了一种三聚体HA蛋白,该蛋白在哺乳动物真核系统中表达并通过Ni亲和层析纯化。动物免疫后,生成并表征了九种 HA 特异性单克隆抗体。
核心方法
通过间接酶联免疫吸附测定 (ELISA)、斑点印迹、蛋白质印迹和间接免疫荧光测定 (IFA),使用生成的单克隆抗体绘制 HA 上的 B 细胞表位。利用生物信息学进一步分析表位区域的空间分布和结构特征。
主要结果
Six previously unreported linear B-cell epitopes were identified: 21GNDNSTATL29, 41IVKTITNDR49, 116YDVPDYASL124, 382DLKSTQAAI390, 458SEMNKLFEK466, and 488KCDNACIGS496. Bioinformatics analysis revealed their spatial distribution and structural characteristics on HA.
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为扩大了目前对 HA 表位的理解,并为开发亚基 疫苗 和高灵敏度检测方法提供了理论基础。新颖之处在于 HA 蛋白上六个新的线性 B 细胞表位的鉴定。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为改进的流感亚基 疫苗 的设计和针对新鉴定的表位的诊断测定提供信息。它们可以增强 疫苗 的功效,并能够更准确地检测循环流感病毒株。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其依赖体外表位作图和动物源性单克隆抗体而受到限制,这可能无法完全反映人类 免疫应答。由于缺乏人体临床验证且表位表征范围有限,证据强度中等。

Identification and computational analysis of B-cell epitopes on the hemagglutinin protein of the H3N2 influenza virus.

Authors: Mengyuan Guo, Xue Wang, Jingming Zhou, Yumei Chen, Chao Liang, Haili Wang, Sixuan Wu, Xifang Zhu, Aiping Wang
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus (IAV) is a highly infectious enveloped RNA virus that primarily causes respiratory disease. Continuous antigenic variation in influenza virus hemagglutinin (HA) is a major driver of influenza pandemics. Therefore, precise identification of B-cell epitopes within HA is essential for developing diagnostic tools and vaccine candidates that can help limit further influenza spread. In the present study, an integrated experimental and bioinformatics strategy was used. A trimeric HA protein was rationally designed to improve antigenicity and immunogenicity, expressed in a mammalian eukaryotic expression system, and purified by Ni-affinity chromatography. After animal immunization, nine HA-specific monoclonal antibodies (mAbs), namely 5D5, 6B8, 9E7, 13F8, 14C3, 15F6, 16B5, 17H7, and 20C5, were generated. These mAbs specifically recognized HA protein in western blotting and indirect immunofluorescence assays (IFA). B-cell epitopes on HA were subsequently mapped using these mAbs by indirect enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA), dot-blot, western blotting, and IFA. Six previously unreported linear B-cell epitopes were identified: 21 GNDNSTATL 29 , 41 IVKTITNDR 49 , 116 YDVPDYASL 124 , 382 DLKSTQAAI 390 , 458 SEMNKLFEK 466 , and 488 KCDNACIGS 496 . Bioinformatics analysis further revealed the spatial distribution and structural characteristics of these epitope regions on HA. These findings expand the current understanding of HA epitopes and provide a theoretical basis for developing IAV subunit vaccines and highly sensitive detection methods.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A virus causes seasonal respiratory disease and its hemagglutinin (HA) protein undergoes continuous antigenic variation, driving pandemics. The study was needed because precise identification of B-cell epitopes within HA is essential for developing diagnostic tools and vaccine candidates to limit influenza spread.
Design and population
The study used an integrated experimental and bioinformatics strategy to design a trimeric HA protein, which was expressed in a mammalian eukaryotic system and purified by Ni-affinity chromatography. After animal immunization, nine HA-specific monoclonal antibodies were generated and characterized.
Core methods
B-cell epitopes on HA were mapped using the generated monoclonal antibodies through indirect enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA), dot-blot, western blotting, and indirect immunofluorescence assays (IFA). The spatial distribution and structural characteristics of epitope regions were further analyzed using bioinformatics.
Main results
Six previously unreported linear B-cell epitopes were identified: 21GNDNSTATL29, 41IVKTITNDR49, 116YDVPDYASL124, 382DLKSTQAAI390, 458SEMNKLFEK466, and 488KCDNACIGS496. Bioinformatics analysis revealed their spatial distribution and structural characteristics on HA.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as expanding current understanding of HA epitopes and providing a theoretical basis for developing subunit vaccines and highly sensitive detection methods. The novelty lies in the identification of six new linear B-cell epitopes on the HA protein.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings can inform the design of improved influenza subunit vaccines and diagnostic assays by targeting newly identified epitopes. They may enhance vaccine efficacy and enable more accurate detection of circulating influenza strains.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on in vitro epitope mapping and animal-derived monoclonal antibodies, which may not fully reflect human immune responses. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of human clinical validation and limited scope of epitope characterization.

初级保健中病毒性呼吸道感染的基于探针的宏基因组前哨 监测:一项前瞻性、全国性试点研究。

Probe-based metagenomic sentinel surveillance of viral respiratory infections in primary care: a prospective, national, pilot study.
作者： Kees Mourik, Igor Sidorov, Erin Meijers, Sharon van den Brink, Sanne Bos, Lynn Aarts, Nithya Kuttiyarthu Veetil, Stefan A Boers, Dirk Eggink, Adam Meijer
摘要中文翻译背景:随着宏基因组学在临床诊断中的引入以及病原体基因组学在国家监测中的作用不断扩大,有利于这些方法在公共卫生监测中进一步成熟的条件。在这项研究中,我们的目的是通过全科医生 (GP) 网络在全国哨兵 监测 中试点使用基于探针的宏基因组学,并在初级保健中对预期和新出现的呼吸道病毒进行基因组表征。方法:这项前瞻性试点研究纳入了 2025 年 1 月 10 日至 2025 年 4 月 25 日期间参加荷兰全国前哨病毒学 监测 的全科医生诊所的急性呼吸道疾病患者。在预定的选择日,通过使用针对 15 488 株病毒株的探针进行平行宏基因组测试,对 90-100 份鼻咽和口咽拭子样本进行了分析。人类和动物病毒。将结果与标准两层 监测 策略进行比较,该策略包括针对 17 种病毒的 PCR,然后对流感病毒、SARS-CoV-2 和呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 进行基于扩增子的纳米孔全 基因组 测序。主要成果是在标准筛查范围内和之外对病毒进行检测和深入的基因组表征。分析了敏感性、特异性、阳性和阴性预测值以及 基因组 覆盖率。结果:纳入 93 名患者,中位年龄 51 岁(IQR 33-67);55 名 (59%) 为女性,38 名 (41%) 为男性。总体而言,通过基于 PCR 的标准筛查,74 份 (80%) 样本检测呈阳性。宏基因组 监测 在 88 个 (95%) 样本中检测到病毒,包括已知会建立潜伏感染的 DNA 病毒。对于常规 PCR 靶向的病毒,宏基因组学显示的汇总敏感性为 93·8% (95% CI 88·1-98·7),特异性为 99·8% (95% CI 99·5-100·0),阳性预测值为 96·2% (95% CI 91·8-100·0),阴性预测值为 99·7% (95% CI) 91·8-100·0),基因组 覆盖率中位数为 99·4% (IQR 96·9-99·9%)。宏基因组数据能够同时对当前基于扩增子的 监测 靶向的循环病毒进行全基因组表征,包括流感病毒(35 次检测中的成功率为 30 [86%])和 RSV(7 次检测中的 6 次 [86%]),以及针对研究期间流行病学发现而通过全 基因组 测序进行事后表征的病毒(人类偏肺病毒 [hMPV]、十种中的五种[50·0%]),以及非靶向病毒,例如腺病毒。这些数据有助于对重配 甲型H3N2流感病毒 流感病毒、潜在的 疫苗 逃逸突变体、流感 甲型H3N2流感病毒 药物和 RSV 单克隆抗体的易感性标记以及人 A(H1N2)v 流感病毒感染进行表征。解释:此处显示的跨异质循环病毒的技术上稳健且全面的性能支持在高 患病率 环境中进行更大型前瞻性研究的评估,在这些环境中实施可能是最具成本效益的。基于探针的富集策略的进一步优化可以减少周转时间并促进融入常规公共卫生 监测。资助:荷兰卫生研究与发展组织 (ZonMw) 以及卫生、福利和体育部 (VWS)。

问题与背景
随着宏基因组学在临床诊断中的引入以及病原体基因组学在国家监测中的作用不断扩大,有利于这些方法在公共卫生监测中进一步成熟的条件。

对于常规 PCR 靶向的病毒,宏基因组学显示的汇总敏感性为 93·8% (95% CI 88·1-98·7),特异性为 99·8% (95% CI 99·5-100·0),阳性预测值为 96·2% (95% CI 91·8-100·0),阴性预测值为 99·7% (95% CI) 91·8-100·0),基因组 覆盖率中位数为 99·4% (IQR 96·9-99·9%)。这里显示的跨异质循环病毒的技术上稳健和全面的性能支持在高 患病率 环境中进行更大规模的前瞻性研究中的评估,在这些环境中实施可能是最具成本效益的。

Probe-based metagenomic sentinel surveillance of viral respiratory infections in primary care: a prospective, national, pilot study.

Authors: Kees Mourik, Igor Sidorov, Erin Meijers, Sharon van den Brink, Sanne Bos, Lynn Aarts, Nithya Kuttiyarthu Veetil, Stefan A Boers, Dirk Eggink, Adam Meijer
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: With the introduction of metagenomics in clinical diagnostics unfolding and the expanding role of pathogen genomics in national surveillance, conditions are favourable for the further maturation of these approaches in public health surveillance. In this study, we aimed to pilot the use of probe-based metagenomics for nationwide sentinel surveillance through general practitioner (GP) networks and for the genomic characterisation of both anticipated and emerging respiratory viruses in primary care. METHODS: This prospective, pilot study included patients with acute respiratory illness attending GP practices participating in nationwide sentinel virological surveillance in the Netherlands, from Jan 10, 2025, to April 25, 2025. On predefined selection days, 90-100 combined nasopharyngeal and oropharyngeal swab specimens were analysed through parallel metagenomic testing using probes targeting 15 488 strains of human and animal viruses. Results were compared with a standard two-tiered surveillance strategy comprising PCR targeting 17 viruses, followed by amplicon-based nanopore whole-genome sequencing of influenza viruses, SARS-CoV-2, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). The primary outcome was detection and in-depth genomic characterisation of viruses within and beyond the scope of standard screening. Sensitivity, specificity, positive and negative predictive values, and genome coverage were analysed. FINDINGS: 93 patients were included, with a median age of 51 years (IQR 33-67); 55 (59%) were women and 38 (41%) were men. Overall, 74 (80%) specimens tested positive through standard PCR-based screening. Metagenomic surveillance detected viruses in 88 (95%) specimens, including DNA viruses known to establish latent infections. For viruses targeted by routine PCR, metagenomics showed a pooled sensitivity of 93·8% (95% CI 88·1-98·7), specificity of 99·8% (95% CI 99·5-100·0), positive predictive value of 96·2% (95% CI 91·8-100·0), and negative predictive value of 99·7% (95% CI 91·8-100·0), with a median genome coverage of 99·4% (IQR 96·9-99·9%). Metagenomic data enabled simultaneous full genomic characterisation of circulating viruses targeted by current amplicon-based surveillance, including influenza viruses (success rate 30 [86%] of 35 detections) and RSV (six [86%] of seven), and of viruses that were post hoc characterised by whole-genome sequencing in response to epidemiological findings during the study period (human metapneumoviruses [hMPV], five [50·0%] of ten), and non-targeted viruses such as adenoviruses. The data facilitated characterisation of a reassortant A(H3N2) influenza virus, potential vaccine escape mutants, markers of susceptibility to influenza antiviral drugs and RSV monoclonal antibodies, and a human A(H1N2)v influenza virus infection. INTERPRETATION: The technically robust and comprehensive performance across heterogeneous circulating viruses shown here supports evaluation in larger prospective studies in high-prevalence settings, where implementation might be most cost-efficient. Further optimisation of probe-based enrichment strategies could reduce turnaround time and facilitate integration into routine public health surveillance. FUNDING: The Netherlands Organisation for Health Research and Development (ZonMw), and the Ministry of Health, Welfare and Sport (VWS).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
With the introduction of metagenomics in clinical diagnostics unfolding and the expanding role of pathogen genomics in national surveillance, conditions are favourable for the further maturation of these approaches in public health surveillance.
Design and population
This prospective, pilot study included patients with acute respiratory illness attending GP practices participating in nationwide sentinel virological surveillance in the Netherlands, from Jan 10, 2025, to April 25, 2025.
Core methods

Results were compared with a standard two-tiered surveillance strategy comprising PCR targeting 17 viruses, followed by amplicon-based nanopore whole-genome sequencing of influenza viruses, SARS-CoV-2, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV).
Main results
93 patients were included, with a median age of 51 years (IQR 33-67); 55 (59%) were women and 38 (41%) were men. Overall, 74 (80%) specimens tested positive through standard PCR-based screening.
Interpretation and novelty
Metagenomic surveillance detected viruses in 88 (95%) specimens, including DNA viruses known to establish latent infections. The data facilitated characterisation of a reassortant A(H3N2) influenza virus, potential vaccine escape mutants, markers of susceptibility to influenza antiviral drugs and RSV monoclonal antibodies, and a human A(H1N1)pdm09 influenza virus infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
Further optimisation of probe-based enrichment strategies could reduce turnaround time and facilitate integration into routine public health surveillance.
Limitations and evidence strength
For viruses targeted by routine PCR, metagenomics showed a pooled sensitivity of 93·8% (95% CI 88·1-98·7), specificity of 99·8% (95% CI 99·5-100·0), positive predictive value of 96·2% (95% CI 91·8-100·0), and negative predictive value of 99·7% (95% CI 91·8-100·0), with a median genome coverage of 99·4% (IQR 96·9-99·9%). The technically robust and comprehensive performance across heterogeneous circulating viruses shown here supports evaluation in larger prospective studies in high-prevalence settings, where implementation might be most cost-efficient.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

住院流感阳性患者临床和实验室标志物的预后价值:基于 ROC 的风险分层的 队列研究 回顾性研究。

Prognostic Value of Clinical and Laboratory Markers in Hospitalized Influenza-Positive Patients: A Retrospective Cohort Study With ROC-Based Risk Stratification.
作者： Mohsen Daneshmand, Arefeh Ebrahimian Shiadeh, Farzane Sadeghi, Hemmat Gholinia, Masomeh Bayani
摘要中文翻译背景与目的:季节性流感 仍在全球造成大量发病与死亡。识别可靠的临床与辅助检查预测因子,有助于早期分诊与管理。本研究旨在评估与甲型流感病毒阳性患者住院时间延长、ICU收治及院内 死亡率 相关的临床与辅助检查因素。方法:本回顾性研究纳入2022-2023年秋冬季在伊朗巴博尔阿亚图拉·鲁哈尼医院收治的294例RT-PCR确诊甲型流感病例。收集人口学、临床与实验室数据。主要结局为住院时间延长(>5天)、ICU收治及院内死亡。采用Logistic回归与ROC分析识别独立预测因子与截断值。结果:住院时间延长发生率为43.5%,ICU收治为8.8%,院内 死亡率 为5.0%。多因素分析显示,吸食鸦片(OR: 5.4)、肾脏疾病(OR: 9.27)及乳酸脱氢酶>1000 U/L(OR: 8.74)与住院时间延长显著相关;乳酸脱氢酶>1000 U/L还可预测ICU收治(OR: 46.9)与院内 死亡率(OR: 21.7);血氧饱和度<93%可增加院内 死亡率 风险(OR: 7.98)。ROC分析显示,乳酸脱氢酶≥633 U/L与C反应蛋白≥88.5 mg/L为预测院内 死亡率 的最佳截断值。结论:在本研究中,乳酸脱氢酶、C反应蛋白、肝酶及血氧饱和度等易获取指标与住院时间延长、ICU收治或院内 死亡率 显著相关,有助于甲型流感住院患者的早期风险分层。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
季节性流感 继续在全球范围内造成大量发病率和 死亡率。确定不良结果的可靠临床和临床旁预测因素可能有助于早期分类和管理。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究分析了 2022 年至 2023 年秋冬期间阿亚图拉鲁哈尼医院(伊朗巴博尔)收治的 294 例 RT-PCR 确诊的流感病例。主要结局是住院时间延长(> 5 天)、入住 ICU 和院内死亡。
核心方法
提取了人口统计、临床和实验室数据。使用逻辑回归和 ROC 分析来确定独立的预测因子和截止值。
主要结果
43.5%的患者出现住院时间延长,8.8%入住ICU,死亡率占5.0%。多变量分析显示,鸦片使用(优势比:5.4)、肾脏疾病(优势比:9.27)及乳酸脱氢酶(LDH)>1000(优势比:8.74)与住院时间延长显著相关。LDH >1000还可预测ICU入住(优势比:46.9)及死亡率(优势比:21.7)。血氧饱和度<93%可增加死亡率风险(优势比:7.98)。ROC分析显示,LDH≥633及C反应蛋白(CRP)≥88.5为预测死亡率的最佳截断值。
解释与创新
在我们的分析中,LDH、CRP、肝酶和氧饱和度等现成标记物与住院时间延长、入住 ICU 或 死亡率 显着相关,可能有助于对住院流感患者进行早期风险分层。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为住院流感患者的早期风险分层策略提供信息,从而有可能改善临床决策和资源分配。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,可能会引入偏差并限制建立因果关系的能力。由于该研究的观察性质,证据是中等的。

Prognostic Value of Clinical and Laboratory Markers in Hospitalized Influenza-Positive Patients: A Retrospective Cohort Study With ROC-Based Risk Stratification.

Authors: Mohsen Daneshmand, Arefeh Ebrahimian Shiadeh, Farzane Sadeghi, Hemmat Gholinia, Masomeh Bayani
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIMS: Seasonal influenza continues to cause substantial morbidity and mortality worldwide. Identifying reliable clinical and paraclinical predictors of adverse outcomes may aid in early triage and management. This study aimed to evaluate clinical and paraclinical factors associated with prolonged hospitalization, ICU admission, and in-hospital mortality in influenza‐positive patients. METHODS: This retrospective study analyzed 294 RT-PCR-confirmed influenza cases admitted to Ayatollah Rouhani Hospital (Babol, Iran) during fall-winter 2022-2023. Demographic, clinical, and laboratory data were extracted. Key outcomes were prolonged hospital stay (> 5 days), ICU admission, and in-hospital death. Logistic regression and ROC analysis were used to identify independent predictors and cutoff values. RESULTS: Prolonged hospitalization occurred in 43.5% of patients, ICU admission in 8.8%, and mortality in 5.0%. In multivariate analysis, opium use (OR: 5.4), renal disease (OR: 9.27), and LDH > 1000 (OR: 8.74) were significantly associated with longer hospital stay. LDH > 1000 also predicted ICU admission (OR: 46.9) and mortality (OR: 21.7). Oxygen saturation < 93% increased mortality risk (OR: 7.98). ROC analysis identified LDH ≥ 633 and CRP ≥ 88.5 as optimal cutoffs for predicting mortality. CONCLUSION: Readily available markers such as LDH, CRP, liver enzymes, and oxygen saturation, which showed significant associations with prolonged hospitalization, ICU admission, or mortality in our analyses, may assist in early risk stratification of hospitalized influenza patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Seasonal influenza continues to cause substantial morbidity and mortality worldwide. Identifying reliable clinical and paraclinical predictors of adverse outcomes may aid in early triage and management.
Design and population
This retrospective study analyzed 294 RT-PCR-confirmed influenza cases admitted to Ayatollah Rouhani Hospital (Babol, Iran) during fall-winter 2022-2023. Key outcomes were prolonged hospital stay (> 5 days), ICU admission, and in-hospital death.
Core methods
Demographic, clinical, and laboratory data were extracted. Logistic regression and ROC analysis were used to identify independent predictors and cutoff values.
Main results
Prolonged hospitalization occurred in 43.5% of patients, ICU admission in 8.8%, and mortality in 5.0%. In multivariate analysis, opium use (OR: 5.4), renal disease (OR: 9.27), and LDH > 1000 (OR: 8.74) were significantly associated with longer hospital stay. LDH > 1000 also predicted ICU admission (OR: 46.9) and mortality (OR: 21.7). Oxygen saturation < 93% increased mortality risk (OR: 7.98). ROC analysis identified LDH ≥ 633 and CRP ≥ 88.5 as optimal cutoffs for predicting mortality.
Interpretation and novelty
Readily available markers such as LDH, CRP, liver enzymes, and oxygen saturation, which showed significant associations with prolonged hospitalization, ICU admission, or mortality in our analyses, may assist in early risk stratification of hospitalized influenza patients.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings may inform early risk stratification strategies for hospitalized influenza patients, potentially improving clinical decision-making and resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective, which may introduce bias and limit the ability to establish causality. The evidence is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

中国广西某城市与流感相关的过量 死亡率 和亚型特异性贡献:广义相加模型分析,2022-2025 年

Influenza-associated excess mortality and subtype-specific contributions in a city in Guangxi, China: a generalized additive model analysis, 2022–2025
作者： Jiayi Zhang, Xin Guo, Yan Yang, Yu Shi, Lingmi Zhou
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流感给全球健康带来了巨大的负担,包括呼吸系统疾病以外的心血管事件风险增加。然而,中国广西有关流感相关过量 死亡率 的区域数据,特别是亚型特异性贡献和高危人群的数据仍然不足。
设计与对象
这项生态时间序列研究收集了广西某市2022年第14周至2025年第13周的每周死亡率数据、A/H3N2、A/H1N1和B/Victoria的流感病毒学监测数据、气象数据和空气污染数据(PM2.5、NO2)。
核心方法
使用具有准泊松分布的广义加性模型 (GAM) 来估计流感相关的过量 死亡率,并根据温度、湿度、PM2.5 和 NO2 进行调整。使用基于 QAIC 的模型比较确定最佳滞后结构,并通过反事实预测将所有流感项设置为零来估计过量的 死亡率。拟合负二项式模型作为敏感性分析。
主要结果
The model explained 77.9% of deviance in all-cause mortality (adjusted R2 = 0.7275). The all-cause excess mortality rate was highest in 2023–2024 (42.36 per 100,000), followed by 2022–2023 (21.00 per 100,000) and 2024–2025 (15.57 per 100,000). Circulatory deaths constituted the majority of excess mortality, and the elderly (≥65 years) accounted for 3,002 excess deaths. A/H3N2 showed the strongest association with mortality (IRR = 1.0017, P = 0.002).
解释与创新
该研究强调,广西与流感相关的 死亡率 过量主要发生在循环系统,集中在老年人中,并且与 A/H3N2 密切相关。这提供了关于亚型特定贡献和高风险群体的新的区域证据,为有针对性的预防策略提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持优先考虑以 A/H3N2 为重点的 疫苗接种 并加强对广西老年人群的循环系统疾病监测。他们还强调需要整合气象和空气污染数据来完善风险评估。
局限与证据强度
生态设计限制了因果推断,并且该研究依赖于汇总数据,而没有对流感感染进行个体层面的临床确认。该模型对于全因 死亡率 的解释力很高,但特定于子类型的估计可能会受到 监测 偏差和未测量的混杂因素的影响,需要谨慎解释。

Influenza-associated excess mortality and subtype-specific contributions in a city in Guangxi, China: a generalized additive model analysis, 2022–2025

Authors: Jiayi Zhang, Xin Guo, Yan Yang, Yu Shi, Lingmi Zhou
Original abstract (English)
Influenza poses a significant global disease burden, with impacts extending beyond the respiratory system to increase cardiovascular event risks. However, regional data on influenza-associated excess mortality, especially regarding subtype-specific contributions and high-risk populations, remain insufficient in Guangxi. This study aimed to explore influenza-associated excess all-cause, respiratory, and circulatory mortality and its population characteristics in a city in Guangxi from 2022 to 2025, providing evidence for targeted prevention and control. Weekly mortality data, influenza virological surveillance data (A/H3N2, A/H1N1, B/Victoria), meteorological data, and air pollution data (PM2. 5, NO2) were collected from the 14th week of 2022 to the 13th week of 2025 in a city in Guangxi. A generalized additive model (GAM) with a quasi-Poisson distribution was used to estimate influenza-associated excess mortality, adjusting for temperature, humidity, PM2.5, and NO2. The optimal lag structure was determined using QAIC-based model comparison. Excess mortality was estimated using counterfactual predictions with all influenza terms set to zero. Negative binomial models were fitted as a sensitivity analysis. The optimal lag structure was 4 weeks for all three influenza subtypes (A/H3N2, A/H1N1, and B/Victoria), 0 weeks for temperature and PM2.5, and 3 weeks for humidity and NO2. The model demonstrated substantial explanatory power for all-cause mortality (adjusted R2 = 0.7275, deviance explained = 77.9%). During the study period, the all-cause excess mortality rate was highest in 2023–2024 (42.36 per 100,000), followed by 2022–2023 (21.00 per 100,000) and 2024–2025 (15.57 per 100,000). Circulatory deaths constituted the majority of excess mortality across all seasons. The elderly population (≥ 65 years) bore the overwhelming majority of excess deaths (3,002 excess deaths). A/H3N2 showed the strongest association with mortality (IRR = 1.0017, P = 0.002). From 2022 to 2025, influenza-associated excess mortality in a city in Guangxi was characterized by a predominance of circulatory diseases and a concentration of risk among the elderly population, with A/H3N2 being the dominant pathogenic subtype.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza imposes a substantial global health burden, including increased risks of cardiovascular events beyond respiratory illness. However, regional data on influenza-associated excess mortality, particularly subtype-specific contributions and high-risk populations, remain insufficient in Guangxi, China.
Design and population
This ecological time-series study collected weekly mortality data, influenza virological surveillance data for A/H3N2, A/H1N1, and B/Victoria, meteorological data, and air pollution data (PM2.5, NO2) from a city in Guangxi between the 14th week of 2022 and the 13th week of 2025.
Core methods
A generalized additive model (GAM) with a quasi-Poisson distribution was used to estimate influenza-associated excess mortality, adjusting for temperature, humidity, PM2.5, and NO2. The optimal lag structure was determined using QAIC-based model comparison, with excess mortality estimated via counterfactual predictions setting all influenza terms to zero. Negative binomial models were fitted as a sensitivity analysis.
Main results
The model explained 77.9% of deviance in all-cause mortality (adjusted R2 = 0.7275). The all-cause excess mortality rate was highest in 2023–2024 (42.36 per 100,000), followed by 2022–2023 (21.00 per 100,000) and 2024–2025 (15.57 per 100,000). Circulatory deaths constituted the majority of excess mortality, and the elderly (≥65 years) accounted for 3,002 excess deaths. A/H3N2 showed the strongest association with mortality (IRR = 1.0017, P = 0.002).
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights that influenza-associated excess mortality in Guangxi is predominantly circulatory, concentrated among the elderly, and most strongly linked to A/H3N2. This provides novel regional evidence on subtype-specific contributions and high-risk groups to inform targeted prevention strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support prioritizing A/H3N2-focused vaccination and enhanced circulatory disease monitoring in elderly populations in Guangxi. They also underscore the need for integrated surveillance incorporating meteorological and air pollution data to refine risk assessment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The ecological design limits causal inference, and the study relies on aggregated data without individual-level clinical confirmation of influenza infection. The model's explanatory power is high for all-cause mortality, but subtype-specific estimates may be influenced by surveillance biases and unmeasured confounders, warranting cautious interpretation.
