儿童阑尾炎中与甲型流感和 I 型超敏反应相关的基因表达模式

Gene expression patterns associated with influenza A and type I hypersensitivity in childhood appendicitis
作者： Marc Reismann, Natalie Kiss, Moritz Markel, Kathrin Hauptmann, Jan F. Svensson, Tomas Wester, STEVEN W. WARMANN
摘要中文翻译背景:甲型流感感染(IAV)和 I 型超敏反应机制均与急性阑尾炎独立相关。特别是与细胞因子白细胞介素 (IL) 13 相关的 I 型超敏反应的表达已被证明在阑尾炎和流感感染中具有重要意义。当前研究的目的是在数学相关性水平上分析各个基因表达的可能关联。方法:我们分析了 29 名 7-17 岁儿童外周血单核细胞中 IAV 相关标志物、超敏反应 I 型相关细胞因子以及炎症标志物 IL-17A 和 C 反应蛋白 (CRP) 的信使 RNA (mRNA) 基因表达,这些儿童于 2019 年 4 月至 8 月在柏林夏里特大学医疗中心因组织学证实的阑尾炎接受手术。采用Spearman相关分析进行研究,相关系数r≥0.5或r≤-0.5代表高度相关,r≥0.7或r≤-0.7代表极高相关。假定 P < 0.01 时具有统计显着性。结果:共有29名患者参与本研究。特别是 IL-13 的表达与 IAV 特异性 抗病毒药物 反应基因的表达呈现高度显着的负相关,范围从 - 0.89 (P < 0.0001) 到 - 0.49 (P = 0.006)。IL-13 与 CRP (r = 0.91,P < 0.0001) 和 IL-17A (r = 0.82,P < 0.0001) 的相关性非常高,证明了强烈的炎症背景。结论:这些发现表明 IAV 感染与儿童急性阑尾炎之间存在联系,暗示阑尾炎病理生理学中的顺序 免疫应答,包括 I 型过敏机制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在分析甲型流感感染相关基因表达与儿童阑尾炎 I 型超敏反应机制之间的可能关联。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 29 名 7-17 岁儿童外周血单核细胞的 mRNA 基因表达,这些儿童于 2019 年 4 月至 8 月期间在柏林夏里特大学医疗中心接受了组织学证实的阑尾炎手术。
核心方法
采用Spearman相关分析研究基因表达之间的统计关系,相关系数r≥0.5或r≤-0.5代表高度相关,r≥0.7或r≤-0.7代表极高相关。假定 P < 0.01 时具有统计显着性。
主要结果
IL-13的表达与IAV特异性抗病毒药物反应基因的表达呈现高度显着的负相关,范围从-0.89(P<0.0001)到-0.49(P=0.006)。IL-13 与 CRP (r = 0.91,P < 0.0001) 和 IL-17A (r = 0.82,P < 0.0001) 的相关性非常高,证明了强烈的炎症背景。
解释与创新
这些发现表明 IAV 感染与儿童急性阑尾炎之间存在联系,暗示阑尾炎病理生理学中的连续 免疫应答,包括 I 型过敏机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于了解儿童阑尾炎病理生理学中涉及的免疫机制,并强调流感感染和超敏反应之间的潜在相互作用。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小和使用相关分析但未确定因果关系的限制。由于该研究的观察性质,证据的强度中等。

Gene expression patterns associated with influenza A and type I hypersensitivity in childhood appendicitis

Authors: Marc Reismann, Natalie Kiss, Moritz Markel, Kathrin Hauptmann, Jan F. Svensson, Tomas Wester, STEVEN W. WARMANN
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Both influenza A infection (IAV) and type I hypersensitivity mechanisms have been independently linked to acute appendicitis. Particularly the expression of type I hypersensitivity associated with cytokine interleukin (IL) 13 has been shown to be of significance in both, appendicitis and influenza infection. The aim of the current study was to analyze possible associations of respective gene expressions at the level of mathematical correlations. METHODS: We analyzed messenger RNA (mRNA) gene expressions of IAV-associated markers, hypersensitivity type I-related cytokines and inflammatory markers IL-17A and c-reactive protein (CRP) in peripheral blood mononuclear cells from 29 children aged 7-17 years, who were operated for histologically confirmed appendicitis at Charité-Universitätsmedizin Berlin between April and August 2019. Statistical relationships between gene expressions were investigated using Spearman's correlation analysis, with a correlation coefficient r ≥ 0.5 or r ≤ - 0.5 representing high, and r ≥ 0.7 or r ≤ - 0.7 very high correlations. Statistical significance was assumed at P < 0.01. RESULTS: A total of 29 patients were involved in this study. Particularly the expression of IL-13 showed highly significant negative correlations with that of IAV-specific antiviral response genes, ranging from - 0.89 (P < 0.0001) to - 0.49 (P = 0.006). A strong inflammatory background was demonstrated by very high correlations of IL-13 with CRP (r = 0.91, P < 0.0001) and IL-17A (r = 0.82, P < 0.0001). CONCLUSION: These findings suggest a connection between IAV infection and acute appendicitis in children, implicating sequential immune responses, including hypersensitivity type I mechanisms, in appendicitis pathophysiology.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to analyze possible associations of gene expressions related to influenza A infection and type I hypersensitivity mechanisms in childhood appendicitis.
Design and population
The study analyzed mRNA gene expressions in peripheral blood mononuclear cells from 29 children aged 7-17 years who were operated on for histologically confirmed appendicitis at Charité-Universitätsmedizin Berlin between April and August 2019.
Core methods
Statistical relationships between gene expressions were investigated using Spearman's correlation analysis, with a correlation coefficient r ≥ 0.5 or r ≤ -0.5 representing high, and r ≥ 0.7 or r ≤ -0.7 very high correlations. Statistical significance was assumed at P < 0.01.
Main results
The expression of IL-13 showed highly significant negative correlations with that of IAV-specific antiviral response genes, ranging from -0.89 (P < 0.0001) to -0.49 (P = 0.006). A strong inflammatory background was demonstrated by very high correlations of IL-13 with CRP (r = 0.91, P < 0.0001) and IL-17A (r = 0.82, P < 0.0001).
Interpretation and novelty
These findings suggest a connection between IAV infection and acute appendicitis in children, implicating sequential immune responses, including hypersensitivity type I mechanisms, in appendicitis pathophysiology.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the understanding of immune mechanisms involved in the pathophysiology of appendicitis in children and highlight potential interactions between influenza infection and hypersensitivity responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its small sample size and the use of correlation analysis without establishing causation. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the observational nature of the study.

基于tNGS的甲型和乙型流感病毒住院儿童临床特征和联合检测概况的比较研究

A tNGS-based comparative study on clinical features and co-detection profiles of hospitalized children with influenza A and B viruses
作者： Chunyun Fu, Qiang Huang, Xiaoying Chen, Haifeng Huang, Junming Lu, Jiaqi Wen, Lishai Mo, Wenting Tang, Jiangyang Zhao, Yili Zhang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述乙型流感病毒(IBV)住院儿童的临床和流行病学特征,并将其与甲型流感病毒(IAV)进行比较。科学和公共卫生问题在于了解这两种病毒在儿科人群中临床表现和共同检测特征的差异。
设计与对象
对 2021 年 4 月至 2024 年 12 月期间住院的急性呼吸道感染 (ARI) 儿童进行了一项基于 tNGS 的比较研究。该研究纳入了 10,143 名儿科住院患者,比较了 IBV 和 IAV 的临床特征和共同检测情况。
核心方法
使用靶向下一代测序 (tNGS) 进行病原体筛查,以确定甲型和乙型流感病毒的存在。进行多变量逻辑回归来分析呼吸支持需求的独立预测因素。
主要结果
IBV was detected in 198 children (2.0%). Children with IBV required less frequent respiratory support than those with IAV (12.6% vs. 18.0%), but multivariable analysis showed that IBV infection independently predicted a higher risk for respiratory support than IAV (aOR = 1.882, 95% CI: 1.134–3.123, p = 0.014).
解释与创新
作者解释说,IBV 和 IAV 显示出相似的年龄分布和相对较高的多种微生物检出率,但多变量分析显示,IBV 感染独立预测比 IAV 更高的呼吸支持需求,这表明 IBV 临床意义的一项新发现。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为临床警惕IBV住院儿科患者的呼吸系统进展提供信息,并强调将IBV视为急性呼吸道感染住院儿童的重要病原体的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到观察设计和潜在混杂因素的限制,但由于使用了靶向下一代测序和多变量逻辑回归分析,证据充足。

A tNGS-based comparative study on clinical features and co-detection profiles of hospitalized children with influenza A and B viruses

Authors: Chunyun Fu, Qiang Huang, Xiaoying Chen, Haifeng Huang, Junming Lu, Jiaqi Wen, Lishai Mo, Wenting Tang, Jiangyang Zhao, Yili Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Objectives This study aimed to characterize the clinical and epidemiological features of hospitalized children with IBV and compare them with those of influenza A virus (IAV). Methods We enrolled 10,143 pediatric inpatients with ARI between April 2021 and December 2024. Pathogen screening was conducted using targeted next-generation sequencing. Multivariable logistic regression was performed to identify independent predictors for respiratory support requirements. Results IBV was detected in 198 children (2.0%). Compared to children with IAV ( n = 325), IBV-positive children showed similar age distribution (median: 38 vs. 42 months, p = 0.464) and similarly high polymicrobial detection rates (96.0% vs. The most frequently co-detected microbes were Haemophilus influenzae , Streptococcus pneumoniae , and Mycoplasma pneumoniae , similar between groups, though the prevalence of H. influenzae was higher in the IBV group than in the IAV group ( p = 0.017). Children with IBV required less frequent respiratory support than those with IAV (12.6% vs. However, multivariable analysis reversed this trend: IBV infection independently predicted a higher risk for respiratory support than IAV (aOR = 1.882, 95% CI: 1.134–3.123, p = 0.014). Younger age (aOR = 0.990, p = 0.009) and female sex (aOR for males = 0.503, p = 0.009) were also independent risk factors. Among IBV cases, 31.3% developed respiratory complications and 46.0% presented with extrapulmonary manifestations. Outcomes were favorable with a median hospitalization of 6 days, low ICU admission rate (4.0%), and no mortality. Conclusion IBV and IAV show similar age distributions and comparably high polymicrobial detection rates in hospitalized children. Importantly, multivariable analysis unmasked that IBV infection independently predicted a higher requirement for respiratory support than IAV, with younger age and female sex identifying particularly vulnerable cohorts. Clinicians should maintain high vigilance for respiratory progression in pediatric IBV inpatients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to characterize the clinical and epidemiological features of hospitalized children with influenza B virus (IBV) and compare them with those of influenza A virus (IAV). The scientific and public-health problem lies in understanding the differences in clinical manifestations and co-detection profiles between these two viruses in pediatric populations.
Design and population
A tNGS-based comparative study was conducted on hospitalized children with acute respiratory infections (ARI) between April 2021 and December 2024. The study enrolled 10,143 pediatric inpatients, comparing clinical features and co-detection profiles of IBV and IAV.
Core methods
Pathogen screening was conducted using targeted next-generation sequencing (tNGS) to identify the presence of influenza A and B viruses. Multivariable logistic regression was performed to analyze independent predictors for respiratory support requirements.
Main results
IBV was detected in 198 children (2.0%). Children with IBV required less frequent respiratory support than those with IAV (12.6% vs. 18.0%), but multivariable analysis showed that IBV infection independently predicted a higher risk for respiratory support than IAV (aOR = 1.882, 95% CI: 1.134–3.123, p = 0.014).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that IBV and IAV show similar age distributions and comparably high polymicrobial detection rates, but multivariable analysis revealed that IBV infection independently predicted a higher requirement for respiratory support than IAV, indicating a novel finding in the clinical implications of IBV.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform clinical vigilance for respiratory progression in pediatric IBV inpatients and highlight the importance of considering IBV as a significant pathogen in hospitalized children with acute respiratory infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and potential confounding factors, but the evidence is strong due to the use of targeted next-generation sequencing and multivariable logistic regression analysis.

H1N1和H3N2流感病毒亚型特异性对巴马小型猪肠道菌群和血清代谢组的影响

Subtype-specific impacts of H1N1 and H3N2 influenza viruses on gut microbiota and serum metabolome in Bama miniature pigs
作者： Chengyi Qi, Bin Fu, Cuilian Yu, Mingshuai Shen, Shumin Chen, Yongan Wang, Kezhou Wang, Zhao Wang
摘要中文翻译摘要:甲型流感病毒,特别是 H1N1 和 H3N2,其致病性和 宿主 相互作用有所不同,但它们对肠道微生物群和 宿主 代谢的影响仍知之甚少。在本研究中,我们通过宏观基因组测序和非靶向代谢组学分析,研究了H1N1和H3N2流感病毒亚型对巴马猪肠道微生物群和血清代谢物的影响。H1N1 和 H3N2 感染均显着改变肠道微生物群组成和血清代谢谱,其中 H3N2 引起更明显的变化。H3N2 感染显着增加了厚壁菌门的相对丰度,减少了拟杆菌门,并进一步减少了肠道微生物群中产生短链脂肪酸的细菌。血清代谢组学分析揭示了 H3N2 感染下更广泛的代谢扰动,包括糖酵解、氨基酸代谢和相关途径的破坏。相关性分析表明特定肠道微生物与血清代谢物之间存在显着关联;例如,普雷沃氏菌与氨基酸生物合成途径呈正相关。这些发现表明,流感病毒亚型可以通过肠道微生物群-代谢物相互作用重塑 宿主 代谢状态,扩大目前对 宿主 对流感感染反应的理解,并为未来的预防和干预策略提供数据驱动的基础。

Subtype-specific impacts of H1N1 and H3N2 influenza viruses on gut microbiota and serum metabolome in Bama miniature pigs

Authors: Chengyi Qi, Bin Fu, Cuilian Yu, Mingshuai Shen, Shumin Chen, Yongan Wang, Kezhou Wang, Zhao Wang
Original abstract (English)
Abstracts Influenza A viruses, particularly H1N1 and H3N2, differ in their pathogenicity and host interactions, yet their impacts on gut microbiota and host metabolism remain poorly characterized. In this study, we investigated the effects of H1N1 and H3N2 influenza virus subtypes on the gut microbiota and serum metabolites of Bama pigs through macrogenomic sequencing and untargeted metabolomics analyses. Both H1N1 and H3N2 infections significantly altered the gut microbiota composition and serum metabolite profiles, with H3N2 inducing more pronounced changes. H3N2 infection markedly increased the relative abundance of Firmicutes, reduced Bacteroidota, and further depleted short-chain fatty acid-producing bacteria in the gut microbiota. Serum metabolomic analysis revealed broader metabolic perturbations under H3N2 infection, including disruptions in glycolysis, amino acid metabolism, and related pathways. Correlation analysis demonstrated significant associations between specific gut microbes and serum metabolites; for example, Prevotella was positively correlated with amino acid biosynthesis pathways. These findings indicate that influenza virus subtypes can remodel host metabolic states through gut microbiota-metabolite interactions, expanding current understanding of host responses to influenza infection and providing a data-driven foundation for future prevention and intervention strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza A viruses, particularly H1N1 and H3N2, differ in their pathogenicity and host interactions, yet their impacts on gut microbiota and host metabolism remain poorly characterized.
Design and population
In this study, we investigated the effects of H1N1 and H3N2 influenza virus subtypes on the gut microbiota and serum metabolites of Bama pigs through macrogenomic sequencing and untargeted metabolomics analyses.
Core methods
Both H1N1 and H3N2 infections significantly altered the gut microbiota composition and serum metabolite profiles, with H3N2 inducing more pronounced changes.
Main results
H3N2 infection markedly increased the relative abundance of Firmicutes, reduced Bacteroidota, and further depleted short-chain fatty acid-producing bacteria in the gut microbiota.
Interpretation and novelty
Serum metabolomic analysis revealed broader metabolic perturbations under H3N2 infection, including disruptions in glycolysis, amino acid metabolism, and related pathways.
Scientific and public-health significance
Correlation analysis demonstrated significant associations between specific gut microbes and serum metabolites; for example, Prevotella was positively correlated with amino acid biosynthesis pathways.
Limitations and evidence strength

因流感住院的成人早期使用阿司匹林与非阿司匹林 NSAID 的临床结果:一项回顾性研究

Clinical outcomes of early aspirin versus non-aspirin NSAID use in adults hospitalized with influenza: A retrospective study
作者： Suk Yin Chan-Colenbrander, Wang, Q., Chan-Colenbrander, S. Y.
摘要中文翻译季节性流感 仍然是全球发病率和 死亡率 的主要原因。尽管神经氨酸酶抑制剂可以改善预后,但与流感相关的死亡仍然存在。我们评估了早期使用阿司匹林 (ASA) 和非阿司匹林非甾体抗炎药 (NSAID) 对因流感住院的成人结局的影响。这项回顾性研究包括 2016 年至 2018 年进入明尼苏达大学医学中心的成年人。连续变量总结为带有四分位数范围 (IQR) 的中位数,分类变量总结为计数和百分比。组比较使用 Wilcoxon 秩和、卡方或 Fisher 精确检验。分析包括病例对照比较、疫苗接种 状态评估以及早期 ASA 或 NSAID 使用的亚组分析。在 2,816 名患者中,320 名患者患有实验室确诊的流感,疫苗接种 在病例中较少见。未接种疫苗的患者入住重症监护病房 (ICU) 的比例较高(23.6% vs. 11.1%;P = 0.003)和通气支持率(15.0% vs. 在接种疫苗的患者中,早期使用 ASA 与年龄较大和较高的住院 死亡率 相关,而早期使用 NSAID 与无院内死亡、更好的一年和三年生存率相关(P < 0.001),并且在未接种疫苗的患者中,使用 ASA 与较低的三年生存率相关(59.1% 对比 79.2%;P = 0.013),而使用 NSAID 则与较少的 ICU 入院率和无心血管或肾脏并发症相关。结果更糟。

这项回顾性研究包括 2016 年至 2018 年入住明尼苏达大学医学中心的成年人。分析包括病例对照比较、疫苗接种 状态评估以及早期 ASA 或 NSAID 使用的亚组分析。

科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为因流感住院的成人患者使用早期非甾体抗炎药与阿司匹林的临床决策提供信息,从而有可能提高生存率并减少并发症。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,可能存在偏差和混杂因素。由于该研究的观察性质,证据强度中等。

Clinical outcomes of early aspirin versus non-aspirin NSAID use in adults hospitalized with influenza: A retrospective study

Authors: Suk Yin Chan-Colenbrander, Wang, Q., Chan-Colenbrander, S. Y.
Original abstract (English)
Seasonal influenza remains a major cause of morbidity and mortality worldwide. Although neuraminidase inhibitors improve outcomes, influenza-related deaths persist. We evaluated the impact of early aspirin (ASA) and non-aspirin nonsteroidal anti-inflammatory drug (NSAID) use on outcomes in adults hospitalized with influenza. This retrospective study included adults admitted to the University of Minnesota Medical Center from 2016 to 2018. Continuous variables were summarized as medians with interquartile ranges (IQRs) and categorical variables as counts and percentages. Group comparisons used Wilcoxon rank-sum, Chi-square, or Fishers exact tests. Analyses included case-control comparisons, assessments by vaccination status, and subgroup analyses by early ASA or NSAID use. Among 2,816 patients, 320 had laboratory-confirmed influenza, with vaccination less common among cases. Unvaccinated patients had higher rates of intensive care unit (ICU) admission (23.6% vs. 11.1%; P = 0.003) and ventilatory support (15.0% vs. In vaccinated patients, early ASA use was associated with older age and higher in-hospital mortality, whereas early NSAID use was associated with no in-hospital deaths, better one- and three-year survival (P < 0.001), and fewer, though not statistically significant, cardiovascular complications. In unvaccinated patients, ASA use was associated with lower three-year survival (59.1% vs. 79.2%; P = 0.013), while NSAID use was associated with fewer ICU admissions and no cardiovascular or renal complications. In both vaccinated and unvaccinated adults hospitalized with influenza, early NSAID use was associated with improved survival and fewer complications, whereas ASA use was associated with worse outcomes.

Question and background
Seasonal influenza remains a major cause of morbidity and mortality worldwide. Although neuraminidase inhibitors improve outcomes, influenza-related deaths persist.
Design and population
This retrospective study included adults admitted to the University of Minnesota Medical Center from 2016 to 2018. Analyses included case-control comparisons, assessments by vaccination status, and subgroup analyses by early ASA or NSAID use.
Core methods
Continuous variables were summarized as medians with interquartile ranges (IQRs) and categorical variables as counts and percentages. Group comparisons used Wilcoxon rank-sum, Chi-square, or Fishers exact tests.
Main results
Among 2,816 patients, 320 had laboratory-confirmed influenza, with vaccination less common among cases. In both vaccinated and unvaccinated adults hospitalized with influenza, early NSAID use was associated with improved survival and fewer complications, whereas ASA use was associated with worse outcomes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that early NSAID use was associated with improved survival and fewer complications, whereas ASA use was associated with worse outcomes in both vaccinated and unvaccinated adults hospitalized with influenza.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform clinical decision-making regarding the use of early NSAIDs versus aspirin in adults hospitalized with influenza, potentially improving survival and reducing complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is retrospective and may be subject to biases and confounding factors. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study.

MDCK-VHG 和 MDCK-SIAT1:用于改善流感病毒传播的设置。

MDCK-VHG and MDCK-SIAT1: set-up for improving influenza virus propagation.
作者： Linda Benincasa, Serena Marchi, Alessandro Manenti, Maria Giovanna Marotta, Ginevra Giglioli, Valentina Salvestroni, Ilaria Manini, Pietro Piu, Margherita Leonardi, Claudia Maria Trombetta
摘要中文翻译鉴定用于流感病毒传播的优化细胞平台仍然是疫苗开发策略和流感病毒研究的核心目标。在世界范围内,MDCK 细胞代表了流感病毒培养最常用的细胞平台。本研究评估了两种 MDCK 衍生细胞系:MDCK-SIAT1(通过 SIAT1 转染产生)和 MDCK-VHG(通过 STAT1 敲除工程改造)。甲型和乙型流感病毒株在亲本 MDCK 和两种修饰细胞系上平行繁殖,并使用血凝 (HA)、基于 ELISA 的微量中和 (MN-ELISA) 和基于比色细胞病变效应的微量中和 (MN-CPE) 测定法评估病毒产量。虽然与亲代 MDCK 细胞相比,使用修饰的细胞系时,乙型流感病毒株没有观察到明显的优势,但 MDCK-VHG 细胞对甲型流感病毒株表现出明显的益处,可以实现更高的病毒滴度并缩短繁殖时间。这些发现表明 MDCK-VHG 细胞是增强甲型流感病毒繁殖的有效替代平台。

设计与对象
本研究评估了两种 MDCK 衍生细胞系:MDCK-SIAT1(通过 SIAT1 转染产生)和 MDCK-VHG(通过 STAT1 敲除工程改造)。
核心方法

MDCK-VHG and MDCK-SIAT1: set-up for improving influenza virus propagation.

Authors: Linda Benincasa, Serena Marchi, Alessandro Manenti, Maria Giovanna Marotta, Ginevra Giglioli, Valentina Salvestroni, Ilaria Manini, Pietro Piu, Margherita Leonardi, Claudia Maria Trombetta
Original abstract (English)
The identification of an optimized cellular platform for the propagation of influenza viruses remains a core aim in vaccine development strategies and study of influenza viruses. Worldwide, MDCK cells represent the most used cellular platform for influenza virus cultivation. This study evaluates two MDCK-derived cell lines: MDCK-SIAT1, generated by transfection with SIAT1, and MDCK-VHG, engineered through STAT1 knockout. Influenza A and B strains were propagated in parallel on parental MDCK and the two modified cell lines, and viral yield was assessed using haemagglutination (HA), ELISA‐based microneutralization (MN‐ELISA), and colorimetric cytopathic effect–based microneutralization (MN‐CPE) assays. While no significant advantages were observed for influenza B strains when using the modified cell lines compared with parental MDCK cells, MDCK‐VHG cells demonstrated a clear benefit for influenza A strains, enabling higher viral titres and reduced propagation times. These findings indicate MDCK‐VHG cells as a valid alternative platform for enhanced influenza A virus propagation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The identification of an optimized cellular platform for the propagation of influenza viruses remains a core aim in vaccine development strategies and study of influenza viruses.
Design and population
This study evaluates two MDCK-derived cell lines: MDCK-SIAT1, generated by transfection with SIAT1, and MDCK-VHG, engineered through STAT1 knockout.
Core methods
Influenza A and B strains were propagated in parallel on parental MDCK and the two modified cell lines, and viral yield was assessed using haemagglutination (HA), ELISA‐based microneutralization (MN‐ELISA), and colorimetric cytopathic effect–based microneutralization (MN‐CPE) assays.
Main results
While no significant advantages were observed for influenza B strains when using the modified cell lines compared with parental MDCK cells, MDCK‐VHG cells demonstrated a clear benefit for influenza A strains, enabling higher viral titres and reduced propagation times.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings indicate MDCK‐VHG cells as a valid alternative platform for enhanced influenza A virus propagation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a specific scientific or public-health implication.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on abstract only. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

2024-2025 年刚果民主共和国农村卫生区 暴发 期间 SARS-COv-2、流感和其他呼吸道病毒共同传播

SARS-COv-2, influenza and other respiratory virus co-circulation during an outbreak in a rural health zone in Democratic Republic of Congo, 2024–2025
作者： Eric Bokabo, H. Situakibanza, Serge Mazamay, Roger Vumilia Kizungu, Mathias Mossoko, Edith Nkwembe, Hélène Broutin
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了对刚果民主共和国 (DRC) SARS-CoV-2 和流感的共同传播和共同感染的有限了解,突显了呼吸道病毒 监测 的知识差距及其对农村地区的影响。
设计与对象
在针对 2024 年至 2025 年期间急性呼吸道感染 (ARI) 病例和死亡增加的健康警报之后,对刚果民主共和国农村卫生区的常规 监测 数据进行了分析。
核心方法
收集口咽和鼻咽拭子,并使用多重 RT-PCR 检测流感、SARS-CoV-2 和其他呼吸道病原体。统计分析包括描述性统计和比较检验(卡方/Fisher 精确检验和 t 检验或韦尔奇检验)。
主要结果
在115个检测样本(疑似病例的2%)中,55个样本(47.8%)至少一种呼吸道病原体呈阳性;30 例(54.5%)检测到流感,15 例(27.3%)检测到 SARS-CoV-2。发现了 11 例合并感染病例,其中包括两例流感与 SARS-CoV-2 感染病例。
解释与创新
该研究记录了呼吸道病毒(特别是流感病毒和 SARS-CoV-2)的共同循环和共同感染,尽管样本量较小,强调刚果民主共和国需要加强常规 监测。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持加强刚果民主共和国的呼吸道病毒 监测 系统,以评估共同循环模式和共同感染的临床影响,特别是针对流感 甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09(检测到的唯一循环亚型)。
局限与证据强度
样本量小(疑似病例的 2%)和缺乏详细的临床结果限制了研究结果的普遍性和稳健性,需要进行低置信度评估。

SARS-COv-2, influenza and other respiratory virus co-circulation during an outbreak in a rural health zone in Democratic Republic of Congo, 2024–2025

Authors: Eric Bokabo, H. Situakibanza, Serge Mazamay, Roger Vumilia Kizungu, Mathias Mossoko, Edith Nkwembe, Hélène Broutin
Original abstract (English)
Little is known about the global impact of co-circulation and co-infection of COVID-19 and influenza in Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC). This study aims at analyzing data on SARS-CoV-2, influenza and other respiratory viruses collected during an outbreak response in the rural Province of Panzi to advocate for the strengthening of respiratory virus surveillance systems. Routinely collected surveillance data were analyzed following a health alert of increases in acute respiratory infection (ARI) cases and deaths. Oropharyngeal and nasopharyngeal swabs were systematically collected and tested for influenza, SARS-CoV-2, and respiratory pathogens using multiplex RT-PCR. Statistical analysis included descriptive statistics and comparative tests (chi-square/Fisher’s exact test and t-test or Welch test). Routine surveillance data yielded a total of 5,781 suspected cases, including 108 deaths. Incidence rates peaked in 2025 for ARI (209 per 100,000 people) and for Influenza-Like Illness (approximately 250 per 100,000 people). Children under one year of age remained the most affected throughout the entire duration of the epidemic. A total of 115 biological samples (approximately 2% of suspected cases) were tested and 55 (47.8%) were positive for at least one respiratory pathogen. Influenza was detected in 30 cases (54.5%), SARS-CoV-2 in 15 cases (27.3%). Median patient age was 5 years (range 0.4–75 years), with 36.4% 1-<5 years. Eleven co-infections, meaning at least 2 virus detected in the same individual, were identified. Among them, co-infection of influenza with SARS-CoV-2 occurred in 2 cases (6.7%), with rhinovirus in 2 cases (6.7%), with parainfluenza type1/2 in 1 case (3.3%), with Bocavirus in 1 case (3.3%), and with adenovirus in 1 case (3.3%). Influenza A (H1N1) pdm09 was the sole circulating influenza subtype. This study documents the co-circulation and co-infection of respiratory viruses, particularly influenza and SARS-CoV-2, despite a small sampling size. These findings emphasize the need to strengthen routine surveillance of respiratory viruses to assess a robust knowledge of co-circulation of respiratory infections and also the clinical impact of the co-infections in DRC.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the limited understanding of co-circulation and co-infection of SARS-CoV-2 and influenza in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), highlighting a knowledge gap in respiratory virus surveillance and impact in rural settings.
Design and population
Routine surveillance data from a rural health zone in DRC were analyzed following a health alert for increased acute respiratory infection (ARI) cases and deaths, covering the period 2024–2025.
Core methods
Oropharyngeal and nasopharyngeal swabs were collected and tested for influenza, SARS-CoV-2, and other respiratory pathogens using multiplex RT-PCR. Statistical analyses included descriptive statistics and comparative tests (chi-square/Fisher’s exact test and t-test or Welch test).
Main results
Among 115 tested samples (2% of suspected cases), 55 (47.8%) were positive for at least one respiratory pathogen; influenza was detected in 30 cases (54.5%) and SARS-CoV-2 in 15 cases (27.3%). Eleven co-infections were identified, including two cases of influenza with SARS-CoV-2.
Interpretation and novelty
The study documents co-circulation and co-infection of respiratory viruses, particularly influenza and SARS-CoV-2, despite a small sampling size, emphasizing the need for strengthened routine surveillance in DRC.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the strengthening of respiratory virus surveillance systems in DRC to assess co-circulation patterns and the clinical impact of co-infections, particularly for influenza A(H1N1)pdm09, the sole circulating subtype detected.
Limitations and evidence strength
The small sample size (2% of suspected cases) and lack of detailed clinical outcomes limit the generalizability and robustness of the findings, warranting a low confidence assessment.

怀孕期间流感疾病与流产的关联以及计划定义的儿童健康监测指标:一项匹配的 队列研究 研究

Associations of Influenza Illness During Pregnancy with Pregnancy Loss and Program-Defined Child Health Monitoring Indicator: A Matched Cohort Study
作者： Yuanyuan Zhang, Yan Shao, Suizan Zhou, Qianlan Wu, Ningning Du, Mingzhi Zhang, Qian Feng, Lin Bao, Yuanyuan Pang, Yayun Tan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了妊娠期间 季节性流感 的公共卫生问题及其对胎儿和婴儿健康结果的潜在影响,填补了将孕产妇流感与妊娠丢失和儿童健康指标联系起来的前瞻性证据的空白。
设计与对象
2015 年至 2018 年在中国苏州进行了一项前瞻性匹配 队列研究 研究,招募了实验室确诊流感的孕妇和未患流感的匹配对照,每周两次进行病情随访,并与健康记录相关联。
核心方法
根据年龄和流产史将实验室确诊的流感病例与对照组进行 1:4 匹配,并使用多变量回归模型来估计与流产和计划定义的儿童健康监测指标的关联。
主要结果
在441名流感孕妇和1764名非流感孕妇中,晚期孕期流产发生率分别为4例(0.9%)vs 1例(0.1%),校正风险比为31.1(95% CI:1.3–756.8,p=0.035);早期孕期流产发生率分别为4例(0.9%)vs 5例(0.3%),校正风险比为2.1(95% CI:0.4–10.4,p=0.374)。
解释与创新
作者将孕产妇流感解释为与晚期流产风险增加以及后代达到计划定义的儿童健康监测指标的几率更高有关,特别是通过超重/肥胖,强调了与儿童健康 监测 指标的新联系。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持目前针对妊娠期间流感 疫苗接种 的建议,以保护孕产妇和婴儿健康,特别是通过减少妊娠晚期流产和改善儿童健康结果。
局限与证据强度
与妊娠丢失的关联是基于很少的事件和较宽的置信区间,需要在更大规模的研究中进行谨慎的解释和确认,表明总体证据强度中等。

Associations of Influenza Illness During Pregnancy with Pregnancy Loss and Program-Defined Child Health Monitoring Indicator: A Matched Cohort Study

Authors: Yuanyuan Zhang, Yan Shao, Suizan Zhou, Qianlan Wu, Ningning Du, Mingzhi Zhang, Qian Feng, Lin Bao, Yuanyuan Pang, Yayun Tan
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: This prospective cohort study assessed the impact of seasonal influenza during pregnancy on fetal and infant health outcomes. Methods: Pregnant women with laboratory-confirmed influenza were matched (1:4) by age and pregnancy loss history with women without influenza from annual cohorts in Suzhou, China, during 2015–2018. Participants underwent twice-weekly follow-up for influenza illness, and medical records were linked to ascertain outcomes. Multivariable regression models were used to estimate associations. Results: We included 441 pregnant women with influenza and 1764 without it. Four (0.9%) in the influenza group had late pregnancy loss, whereas one (0.1%) in the non-influenza group did. Influenza was associated with late pregnancy loss (adjusted hazard ratio [aHR] 31.1, 95% Confidence Interval [CI]: 1.3–756.8, p = 0.035). Four (0.9%) in the influenza group experienced early pregnancy loss, while five (0.3%) in the non-influenza group did. Influenza was not significantly associated with early pregnancy loss (aHR 2.1, 95% CI: 0.4–10.4, p = 0.374). By 8 months of age, infants born to 198 of the 441 mothers (44.9%) in the influenza group met the criteria for the program-defined child health monitoring indicator, compared with infants born to 650 of the 1764 mothers (36.8%) in the non-influenza group. Overweight/obesity was the largest contributor: 142 (32.2%) versus 470 (26.6%). Maternal influenza was associated with higher odds of meeting program-defined child health monitoring indicator in offspring (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] 1.4, 95% CI: 1.1–1.8, p = 0.006), with a significant contribution from overweight/obesity (aOR 1.3, 95% CI: 1.0–1.7, p = 0.045). Conclusions: In this cohort, influenza during pregnancy was associated with an increased risk of late pregnancy loss and program-defined child health monitoring indicator in offspring, particularly overweight/obesity. Because the observed association with pregnancy loss was based on a small number of events and had a wide confidence interval, it should be interpreted cautiously and confirmed in larger studies. These findings are consistent with current recommendations supporting influenza vaccination during pregnancy to protect maternal and infant health.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health problem of seasonal influenza during pregnancy and its potential effects on fetal and infant health outcomes, filling a gap in prospective evidence linking maternal influenza to pregnancy loss and child health indicators.
Design and population
A prospective matched cohort study was conducted in Suzhou, China, from 2015 to 2018, enrolling pregnant women with laboratory-confirmed influenza and matched controls without influenza, followed twice-weekly for illness and linked to health records.
Core methods
Laboratory-confirmed influenza cases were matched 1:4 to controls by age and pregnancy loss history, and multivariable regression models were used to estimate associations with pregnancy loss and a program-defined child health monitoring indicator.
Main results
Among 441 women with influenza and 1,764 without, 4 (0.9%) versus 1 (0.1%) had late pregnancy loss, yielding an adjusted hazard ratio of 31.1 (95% CI: 1.3–756.8, p = 0.035); 4 (0.9%) versus 5 (0.3%) had early pregnancy loss (aHR 2.1, 95% CI: 0.4–10.4, p = 0.374).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted maternal influenza as associated with increased risk of late pregnancy loss and higher odds of offspring meeting a program-defined child health monitoring indicator, particularly via overweight/obesity, highlighting a novel link to child health surveillance metrics.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support current recommendations for influenza vaccination during pregnancy to protect maternal and infant health, particularly by reducing late pregnancy loss and improving child health outcomes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The association with pregnancy loss was based on few events and wide confidence intervals, warranting cautious interpretation and confirmation in larger studies, indicating moderate overall evidence strength.

年轻人和老年人中免疫抑制细胞群和 免疫应答 与流感 疫苗接种 之间的关联

Association Between Immunosuppressive Cell Populations and Immune Response to Influenza Vaccination in Younger and Older Adults
作者： Daniela Di Placido, Antonio Ciaramella, Antonella Riccomi, Claudia Maria Trombetta, Maria Dorrucci, Francesca Farchi, Roberto Giuseppetti, Serena Marchi, Nunzia Sanarico, Patrizio Pezzotti
摘要中文翻译背景:季节性流感 疫苗接种 在个体之间表现出高度可变的有效性,特别是在老年人中,其中与年龄相关的免疫下降损害了保护反应。方法:本研究在 75 名年龄 25-89 岁的个体中评估了 免疫应答 对 2019/2020 四价流感 疫苗 的疗效。在 疫苗接种 之前和之后 4 周测量了流感特异性抗体滴度和病毒中和活​​性,并将参与者分层为高反应者或低反应者。结果:我们观察到,老年人表现出髓源性抑制细胞 (MDSC) 和表达 HLA-DR 和 CD38 的激活调节性 T 细胞 (Treg) 的频率增加,这与免疫抑制活性增强相一致。重要的是,这些免疫调节群体的较高水平与成人和老年参与者的 疫苗 反应性较差相关。此外,老年参与者和低反应者的循环滤泡调节性T细胞(Tfr)升高,而滤泡辅助性T细胞(Tfh)保持不变,导致与抗体产生受损相关的Tfr/Tfh比率增加。然而,在调整可能的混杂因素时,除 CD38+ Treg 细胞外,大多数免疫调节亚群并未与应答者状态保持独立相关。结论:这些发现表明,免疫调节网络虽然对于控制衰老过程中的慢性炎症至关重要,但可能会限制 疫苗 诱导的免疫。识别 MDSC 频率、激活的 Treg 表型和 Tfr/Tfh 平衡等生物标志物可能有助于识别具有不同 疫苗 反应水平的个体,并有助于为未来的个性化 疫苗 策略提供信息。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
季节性流感 疫苗接种 在个体之间表现出高度可变的有效性,特别是在老年人中,其中与年龄相关的免疫下降会损害保护反应。
设计与对象

核心方法
在 疫苗接种 之前和之后 4 周测量流感特异性抗体滴度和病毒中和活​​性。根据这些测量结果,参与者被分为高反应者或低反应者。
主要结果
我们观察到,老年人表现出髓源性抑制细胞 (MDSC) 和表达 HLA-DR 和 CD38 的激活调节性 T 细胞 (Treg) 的频率增加,这与增强的免疫抑制活性一致。这些免疫调节群体的较高水平与成人和老年参与者的 疫苗 反应性较差相关。此外,老年参与者和低反应者的循环滤泡调节性T细胞(Tfr)升高,而滤泡辅助性T细胞(Tfh)保持不变,导致与抗体产生受损相关的Tfr/Tfh比率增加。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,免疫调节网络虽然对于控制衰老过程中的慢性炎症至关重要,但可能会限制 疫苗 诱导的免疫。
科研与公卫意义
识别 MDSC 频率、激活的 Treg 表型和 Tfr/Tfh 平衡等生物标志物可能有助于识别具有不同 疫苗 反应水平的个体,并有助于为未来的个性化 疫苗 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
在调整可能的混杂因素时,除 CD38+ Treg 细胞外,大多数免疫调节亚群并未与应答者状态保持独立相关。

Association Between Immunosuppressive Cell Populations and Immune Response to Influenza Vaccination in Younger and Older Adults

Authors: Daniela Di Placido, Antonio Ciaramella, Antonella Riccomi, Claudia Maria Trombetta, Maria Dorrucci, Francesca Farchi, Roberto Giuseppetti, Serena Marchi, Nunzia Sanarico, Patrizio Pezzotti
Original abstract (English)
Background: Seasonal influenza vaccination shows highly variable effectiveness across individuals, particularly in older adults, in whom age-related immune decline compromises protective responses. Methods: This study evaluated immune responses to the 2019/2020 quadrivalent influenza vaccine in 75 individuals aged 25–89 years. Influenza-specific antibody titers and virus-neutralizing activity were measured before and four weeks after vaccination, and participants were stratified as high or low responders. Results: We observed that older individuals exhibited increased frequencies of myeloid-derived suppressor cells (MDSCs) and activated regulatory T cells (Treg) expressing HLA-DR and CD38, consistent with enhanced immunosuppressive activity. Importantly, higher levels of these immunoregulatory populations correlated with poor vaccine responsiveness in both adult and older participants. Additionally, circulating T follicular regulatory (Tfr) cells were elevated in older participants and in low responders, whereas T follicular helper (Tfh) cells remained unchanged, resulting in an increased Tfr/Tfh ratio associated with impaired antibody production. However, when adjusting for possible confounders, most immunoregulatory subsets did not remain independently associated with responder status, except CD38+ Treg cells. Conclusions: These findings suggest that immunoregulatory networks, while essential for controlling chronic inflammation during aging, may limit vaccine-induced immunity. Identifying biomarkers such as MDSC frequency, activated Treg phenotype, and Tfr/Tfh balance may contribute to the identification of individuals with different levels of vaccine responsiveness and could help inform future personalized vaccination strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Seasonal influenza vaccination shows highly variable effectiveness across individuals, particularly in older adults, in whom age-related immune decline compromises protective responses.
Design and population
This study evaluated immune responses to the 2019/2020 quadrivalent influenza vaccine in 75 individuals aged 25–89 years. Participants were stratified as high or low responders based on influenza-specific antibody titers and virus-neutralizing activity measured before and four weeks after vaccination.
Core methods
Influenza-specific antibody titers and virus-neutralizing activity were measured before and four weeks after vaccination. Participants were stratified as high or low responders based on these measurements.
Main results
We observed that older individuals exhibited increased frequencies of myeloid-derived suppressor cells (MDSCs) and activated regulatory T cells (Treg) expressing HLA-DR and CD38, consistent with enhanced immunosuppressive activity. Higher levels of these immunoregulatory populations correlated with poor vaccine responsiveness in both adult and older participants. Additionally, circulating T follicular regulatory (Tfr) cells were elevated in older participants and in low responders, whereas T follicular helper (Tfh) cells remained unchanged, resulting in an increased Tfr/Tfh ratio associated with impaired antibody production.

Limitations and evidence strength

结构建模和实验验证定义了驱动限制和逃逸的 MxA-Thogotovirus 核蛋白界面

Structural modeling and experimental validation define the MxA-Thogotovirus nucleoprotein interface that drives restriction and escape
作者： Chi, L. A., Levy, M., Geiger, R., Malik, H. S., Patel, J. S.
摘要中文翻译人 MxA(粘病毒抗性蛋白 A)宿主 限制因子通过与托戈托病毒 (THOV) 和甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 等正粘病毒的核蛋白结合来抑制它们。尽管 MxA 是在六十多年前就被发现的,但它如何与病毒靶点相互作用仍不清楚。早期使用进化分析和诱变的研究表明,MxA L4 环,尤其是残基 561 处的疏水性芳香族氨基酸,对于结合 THOV 核蛋白 (NP) 和限制 THOV 至关重要。在这里,我们将先前的见解与结构预测方法、分子动力学模拟和实验验证相结合,定义了人类 MxA L4 环与 THOV NP 的结合界面。我们还通过经典的全原子分子动力学模拟评估了 MxA L4-NP 结合的稳定性。我们的模型显示 MxA L4 与 THOV NP 上的表面暴露位点结合,包括先前与病毒逃脱 MxA 限制相关的残基,尽管此信息并未用于指导我们的建模工作。该 MxA-THOV NP 界面与 NP 的 RNA 结合或寡聚化表面不同。我们的分子动力学模拟也与早期数据一致,表明 F561Y 增强了 MxA 与 THOV NP 的结合,而 F561W 减少了它,而 F561V 完全消除了它。基于该模型,我们预测了人类 MxA 或 THOV NP 中的特定变异,这些变异可能导致 宿主 限制或病毒逃逸增加。我们使用病毒微型复制子测定测试了这些预测,以验证我们的模型。我们的努力将指导重要的病毒 监测 研究和基于 MxA 的 监测 的开发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在定义驱动限制和逃逸的 MxA-Thogotovirus 核蛋白界面,因为人类 MxA 宿主 限制因子通过与 Thogotovirus (THOV) 和甲型流感病毒 (IAV) 等正粘病毒的核蛋白结合来抑制其核蛋白。尽管 MxA 是在六十多年前就被发现的,但它如何与病毒靶点相互作用仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究将先前的见解与结构预测方法、分子动力学模拟和实验验证相结合,定义了人类 MxA L4 环与 THOV NP 的结合界面。它通过经典的全原子分子动力学模拟评估了 MxA L4-NP 结合的稳定性。
核心方法
该研究使用结构预测方法、分子动力学模拟和病毒微型复制子测定来测试有关 MxA-THOV NP 相互作用的预测。它还包括早期研究的进化分析和诱变。
主要结果
该模型揭示了 MxA L4 与 THOV NP 表面暴露位点的结合,包括先前与病毒逃脱 MxA 限制相关的残基。该研究预测人类 MxA 或 THOV NP 中的特定变异可能导致 宿主 限制或病毒逃逸增加。
解释与创新
这些发现提供了对 MxA-THOV NP 界面的详细了解,该界面不同于 NP 的 RNA 结合或寡聚化表面。该信息并未用于指导建模工作,突显了结果的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果将指导重要的病毒 监测 研究和基于 MxA 的 抗病毒药物 的开发,提供对 宿主 与病毒相互作用的见解,从而为未来的治疗策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,研究结果得到分子动力学模拟和实验验证的支持,这为结论提供了适度的可信度。

Structural modeling and experimental validation define the MxA-Thogotovirus nucleoprotein interface that drives restriction and escape

Authors: Chi, L. A., Levy, M., Geiger, R., Malik, H. S., Patel, J. S.
Original abstract (English)
The human MxA (myxovirus resistance protein A) host restriction factor inhibits orthomyxoviruses, such as Thogotovirus (THOV) and influenza A virus (IAV), by binding to their nucleoproteins. Despite being discovered over six decades ago, how MxA interacts with viral targets remains unclear. Earlier studies using evolutionary analysis and mutagenesis showed that the MxA L4 loop, especially a hydrophobic aromatic amino acid at residue 561, is crucial for binding THOV nucleoprotein (NP) and restricting THOV. Here, we combined previous insights with structure prediction methods, molecular dynamics simulations, and experimental validation to define the human MxA L4 loop binding interface to THOV NP. We also evaluated the stability of MxA L4-NP binding through classical all-atom molecular dynamics simulations. Our model revealed MxA L4 binding to a surface-exposed site on THOV NP, including residues previously linked to viral escape from MxA restriction, even though this information was not used to guide our modeling efforts. This MxA-THOV NP interface is distinct from NP's RNA-binding or oligomerization surfaces. Our molecular dynamics simulations also agree with earlier data indicating that F561Y enhances MxA binding to THOV NP, whereas F561W reduces it and F561V ablates it entirely. Based on this model, we predicted specific variants in human MxA or THOV NP that could result in increased host restriction or viral escape. We tested these predictions using a viral minireplicon assay to validate our model. Our efforts will guide vital viral surveillance studies and the development of MxA-based antivirals.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to define the MxA-Thogotovirus nucleoprotein interface that drives restriction and escape, as the human MxA host restriction factor inhibits orthomyxoviruses, such as Thogotovirus (THOV) and influenza A virus (IAV), by binding to their nucleoproteins. Despite being discovered over six decades ago, how MxA interacts with viral targets remains unclear.
Design and population
The study combined previous insights with structure prediction methods, molecular dynamics simulations, and experimental validation to define the human MxA L4 loop binding interface to THOV NP. It evaluated the stability of MxA L4-NP binding through classical all-atom molecular dynamics simulations.
Core methods
The study used structure prediction methods, molecular dynamics simulations, and a viral minireplicon assay to test predictions about MxA-THOV NP interactions. It also included evolutionary analysis and mutagenesis from earlier studies.
Main results
The model revealed MxA L4 binding to a surface-exposed site on THOV NP, including residues previously linked to viral escape from MxA restriction. The study predicted specific variants in human MxA or THOV NP that could result in increased host restriction or viral escape.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide a detailed understanding of the MxA-THOV NP interface, which is distinct from NP's RNA-binding or oligomerization surfaces. This information was not used to guide the modeling efforts, highlighting the novelty of the results.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results will guide vital viral surveillance studies and the development of MxA-based antivirals, offering insights into host-virus interactions that could inform future therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, and the findings are supported by molecular dynamics simulations and experimental validation, which provide moderate confidence in the conclusions.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

流感。

Influenza.
作者： Gabriele Neumann, Benjamin J. Cowling, Hualan Chen, Silke Stertz, Balaji Manicassamy, Ian G. Barr, Timothy M. Uyeki, Yoshihiro Kawaoka
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
季节性流感 的临床表现通常是一种突发的、无并发症的急性呼吸道疾病,但已完全康复;然而,它可能会导致高危个体出现严重的、危及生命的并发症。
证据基础与方法
诊断 主要依赖于高度灵敏的基于 PCR 的方法和多重快速抗原测试。
共识与结论
宿主 防御涉及分层的先天反应,该反应被蛋白质(例如非结构蛋白 1)拮抗,以及强大的适应性免疫,包括针对保守内部蛋白质的细胞毒性 CD8 + T 细胞和产生中和抗体的 B 细胞。
争议与缺口
研究重点包括开发改进的 疫苗 以及更好地了解 传播 从动物到人类的流感病毒。
科研与实践启示
预防涉及每年更新的季节性 疫苗(包括灭活、减毒活和基于蛋白质的 疫苗),而 治疗 则依赖于早期使用神经氨酸酶和聚合酶酸性蛋白抑制剂。

Influenza.

Authors: Gabriele Neumann, Benjamin J. Cowling, Hualan Chen, Silke Stertz, Balaji Manicassamy, Ian G. Barr, Timothy M. Uyeki, Yoshihiro Kawaoka
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A and B viruses are considerable public health threats. Annual seasonal epidemics are driven by antigenic drift and cause an estimated 3-5 million severe cases and 290,000-650,000 deaths annually. The clinical presentation of seasonal influenza is typically an abrupt, uncomplicated acute respiratory illness with full recovery; however, it can lead to severe, life-threatening complications in individuals at high risk. Antigenic shift caused by reassortment of a seasonal influenza A virus with avian and/or swine influenza A viruses has led to unpredictable pandemics. The successful cross-species transmission of influenza A viruses requires key viral adaptations, including changes in receptor specificity and compatibility with host factors such as ANP32A. Host defence involves a layered innate response, which is antagonized by proteins, such as non-structural protein 1, and a robust adaptive immunity comprising cytotoxic CD8 + T cells targeting conserved internal proteins and B cells producing neutralizing antibodies. Diagnosis primarily depends on highly sensitive PCR-based methods and multiplexed rapid antigen tests. Prevention involves annually updated seasonal vaccines (including inactivated, live attenuated and protein-based vaccines), while treatment relies on early use of neuraminidase and polymerase acidic protein inhibitors. Research priorities include the development of improved vaccines and a better understanding of influenza virus transmission from animals to humans.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The clinical presentation of seasonal influenza is typically an abrupt, uncomplicated acute respiratory illness with full recovery; however, it can lead to severe, life-threatening complications in individuals at high risk.
Evidence base and method
Diagnosis primarily depends on highly sensitive PCR-based methods and multiplexed rapid antigen tests.
Consensus and conclusions
Host defence involves a layered innate response, which is antagonized by proteins, such as non-structural protein 1, and a robust adaptive immunity comprising cytotoxic CD8 + T cells targeting conserved internal proteins and B cells producing neutralizing antibodies.
Controversies and gaps
Research priorities include the development of improved vaccines and a better understanding of influenza virus transmission from animals to humans.
Research and practice implications
Prevention involves annually updated seasonal vaccines (including inactivated, live attenuated and protein-based vaccines), while treatment relies on early use of neuraminidase and polymerase acidic protein inhibitors.

破译乙型流感病毒衍生肽及其通过 HLA I 类分子的呈递。

Deciphering influenza B virus-derived peptides and their presentation by HLA class I molecules.
作者： Janesha C Maddumage, Georgia R Dow, Lawton D Murdolo, Samuel Liwei Leong, Demetra S M Chatzileontiadou, Emma J Grant, Stephanie Gras
摘要中文翻译尽管流感 疫苗 已广泛使用,但流感仍然每年导致显着的发病率和 死亡率。疫苗 通常会诱导体液介导的针对快速突变表面糖蛋白的保护,因此需要每年更新和施用它们。相比之下,可以控制和清除病毒感染的CD8+ T细胞可以识别更保守的病毒表位。因此,人们对了解 CD8+ T 细胞对流感病毒的反应有很大兴趣,以开发未来的 疫苗 和治疗方法。尽管甲型流感病毒 (FLUAV) 和甲型流感病毒 (FLUBV) 在人类中共同传播,并且都导致季节性流行,但有关 CD8+ T 细胞对 FLUBV 反应的数据有限。这种知识差距涵盖了免疫学和分子学方面的见解。在本综述中,我们总结了目前对 FLUBV 衍生的 CD8+ T 细胞表位在细胞和分子水平上的了解,并与 FLUAV 进行了比较。总的来说,这凸显了有关 FLUBV 的可用数据有限,尽管它在人类流感感染中发挥着重要作用。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述检查了人类 季节性流感 B 病毒 (FLUBV),重点是 CD8+ T 细胞对保守表位的反应,与甲型流感病毒 (FLUAV) 进行对比,并解决了这些反应如何为未来的 疫苗 和治疗提供信息的核心问题。
证据基础与方法
该综述综合了细胞和分子水平上 FLUBV 衍生的 CD8+ T 细胞表位的最新知识,明确比较了 FLUBV 与 FLUAV,并强调了有关 FLUBV 的可用数据有限,尽管它在人类流感感染中发挥着作用。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,CD8+ T 细胞比体液反应识别更保守的病毒表位,从而提供了更广泛保护的潜力,但指出与 FLUAV 相比,FLUBV 特异性 CD8+ T 细胞表位数据仍然稀疏。
争议与缺口
该综述指出了关于 FLUBV 衍生的 CD8+ T 细胞表位的重大知识差距,涵盖免疫学和分子见解,并强调在这方面缺乏 FLUBV 和 FLUAV 之间的比较数据。
科研与实践启示
该综述优先研究表征 FLUBV 衍生的 CD8+ T 细胞表位及其 HLA I 类分子的呈递,旨在指导 疫苗 和利用保守 T 细胞反应的疗法的开发。

Deciphering influenza B virus-derived peptides and their presentation by HLA class I molecules.

Authors: Janesha C Maddumage, Georgia R Dow, Lawton D Murdolo, Samuel Liwei Leong, Demetra S M Chatzileontiadou, Emma J Grant, Stephanie Gras
Original abstract (English)
Despite influenza vaccines being widely available, influenza still causes significant morbidity and mortality annually. Vaccines typically induce humoral-mediated protection against rapidly mutating surface glycoproteins, necessitating that they be updated and administered each year. In contrast, CD8+ T cells, which can control and clear viral infections, can recognise more conserved viral epitopes. Therefore, there is considerable interest in understanding CD8+ T cell responses to influenza virus for the development of future vaccines and therapeutics. Although Alphainfluenzavirus influenzae (FLUAV) and Betainfluenzavirus influenzae (FLUBV) co-circulate in humans and both contribute to seasonal epidemics, there is limited data regarding CD8+ T cell responses to FLUBV. This knowledge gap spans both immunological and molecular insights. In the present review, we summarise the current knowledge of FLUBV-derived CD8+ T cell epitopes at both cellular and molecular levels, in comparison with FLUAV. Collectively, this highlights the limited data available on FLUBV, despite its significant role in human influenza infections.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines human seasonal influenza B virus (FLUBV) with a focus on CD8+ T cell responses to conserved epitopes, contrasting with influenza A virus (FLUAV) and addressing the central question of how these responses could inform future vaccines and therapeutics.
Evidence base and method
The review synthesizes current knowledge on FLUBV-derived CD8+ T cell epitopes at cellular and molecular levels, explicitly comparing FLUBV to FLUAV, and highlights the limited data available on FLUBV despite its role in human influenza infections.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that CD8+ T cells recognize more conserved viral epitopes than humoral responses, offering potential for broader protection, but notes that FLUBV-specific CD8+ T cell epitope data remain sparse compared to FLUAV.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies a significant knowledge gap regarding FLUBV-derived CD8+ T cell epitopes, spanning both immunological and molecular insights, and emphasizes the lack of comparative data between FLUBV and FLUAV in this context.
Research and practice implications
The review prioritizes research to characterize FLUBV-derived CD8+ T cell epitopes and their presentation by HLA class I molecules, aiming to guide the development of vaccines and therapeutics that leverage conserved T cell responses.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

间质性肺病住院患者符合急性加重标准的流感相关急性呼吸系统恶化:一项单中心回顾性 队列研究 研究

Influenza-associated acute respiratory deterioration fulfilling acute exacerbation criteria in hospitalized patients with interstitial lung disease: a single-center retrospective cohort study
作者： Hiroshi Nakahama, Kazumasa Ogawa, Atsushi Miyamoto, Atsuko Kurosaki, Yui Takahashi, Takahiro Mitsumura, Shigeo Hanada, Meiyo Tamaoka
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 间质性肺疾病 (ILD) 的急性加重 (AE) 危及生命,但实验室确诊的流感感染后符合 AE 标准的急性呼吸系统恶化的数据有限。目前的研究调查了住院 ILD 患者经实验室确诊流感后的 发病率、临床特征和此类事件的结果。方法 我们回顾性分析了 2014 年 4 月至 2019 年 4 月期间在虎之门医院因流感住院的既往 ILD 成人。通过快速诊断测试确诊流感。两名肺科医师和一名放射科医师确认符合 AE 标准。比较有和没有 AE 标准事件的患者的基线特征和结果。组间比较采用Mann-Whitney U检验或Fisher精确检验,生存分析采用Kaplan-Meier法和对数秩检验。结果 研究期间,927 名因 ILD 住院的患者中,47 名患有实验室确诊的流感,其中 10 名患者 (21.3%) 出现了 AE 标准事件。从流感 诊断 到事件发作的中位间隔为 5.0 天。与没有 AE 标准事件的患者相比,发生 AE 标准事件的患者预测用力肺活量百分比较低(70.0% 对比 88.0%,p = 0.041),且 ILD-GAP 阶段较高。发生 AE 标准事件的患者的住院 死亡率 率为 30.0%。在未经调整的分析中,发生 AE 标准事件的患者的流感 诊断 总生存期似乎较短。结论 实验室确诊的流感后,大约五分之一的 ILD 住院患者出现符合 AE 标准的急性呼吸系统恶化,并且与高住院 死亡率 相关。未经调整的分析还表明这些患者的总生存期较短。这些发现应被视为探索性和假设生成,并需要在更大规模的多中心研究中得到证实。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该研究检查了实验室确诊的 发病率 感染后患有间质性肺疾病 (ILD) 的住院患者的 发病率 和满足急性加重 (AE) 标准的急性呼吸系统恶化的结果,重点关注这一弱势群体中此类事件的临床负担和预后。
证据基础与方法
该评价是一项单中心回顾性 队列研究 研究,研究对象为 2014 年 4 月至 2019 年 4 月期间在虎之门医院因流感住院的既往 ILD 成人。流感通过快速诊断测试得到确认,并且由两名肺科医师和一名放射科医师判定是否符合 AE 标准。使用 Mann–Whitney U 检验、Fisher 精确检验、Kaplan–Meier 方法和对数秩检验比较基线特征和结果。
共识与结论
实验室确诊的流感后,大约五分之一 (21.3%) 的 ILD 住院患者出现符合 AE 标准的急性呼吸系统恶化。与没有发生 AE 标准事件的患者相比,发生 AE 标准事件的患者的预测用力肺活量百分比显着较低(70.0% 对比 88.0%,p = 0.041),并且 ILD-GAP 阶段较高。发生 AE 标准事件的患者的住院 诊断 率为 30.0%,在未经调整的分析中,流感 诊断 的总生存期似乎较短。
争议与缺口
研究结果是探索性的和假设性的,需要在更大规模的多中心研究中得到证实。未经调整的生存分析限制了因果推断,并且对流感确认的快速诊断测试的依赖可能会引入错误分类偏差。缺乏对混杂因素的多变量调整进一步限制了观察到的关联的稳健性。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应优先考虑进行多变量调整的多中心前瞻性研究,以验证观察到的关联并阐明流感与 ILD 中 AE 的联系机制。临床上,考虑到院内 监测 较高且发生 AE 标准事件的患者生存期较短,可能需要加强 ILD 患者流感的 监测 和早期干预策略。

Influenza-associated acute respiratory deterioration fulfilling acute exacerbation criteria in hospitalized patients with interstitial lung disease: a single-center retrospective cohort study

Authors: Hiroshi Nakahama, Kazumasa Ogawa, Atsushi Miyamoto, Atsuko Kurosaki, Yui Takahashi, Takahiro Mitsumura, Shigeo Hanada, Meiyo Tamaoka
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Acute exacerbation (AE) of interstitial lung disease (ILD) is life threatening, but data on acute respiratory deterioration fulfilling AE criteria after laboratory-confirmed influenza infection are limited. The current study investigated the incidence, clinical features, and outcomes of such events after laboratory-confirmed influenza in hospitalized patients with ILD. Methods We retrospectively reviewed adults with pre-existing ILD who were hospitalized with influenza at Toranomon Hospital between April 2014 and April 2019. Influenza was confirmed by rapid diagnostic tests. Fulfillment of the AE criteria was confirmed by two pulmonologists and one radiologist. Baseline characteristics and outcomes were compared between patients with and without AE-criteria events. The Mann–Whitney U test or Fisher’s exact test were used for between-group comparisons, and the Kaplan–Meier method and the log-rank test were used for survival analysis. Results Of 927 patients hospitalized with ILD during the study period, 47 had laboratory-confirmed influenza, and 10 of these patients (21.3%) developed AE-criteria events. The median interval from influenza diagnosis to event onset was 5.0 days. Compared with patients without AE-criteria events, those with AE-criteria events had lower percent predicted forced vital capacity (70.0% versus 88.0%, p = 0.041) and higher ILD-GAP stage. The in-hospital mortality rate in patients with AE-criteria events was 30.0%. Overall survival from influenza diagnosis appeared shorter in patients with AE-criteria events in unadjusted analysis. Conclusions Acute respiratory deterioration fulfilling AE criteria occurred in approximately one fifth of hospitalized patients with ILD after laboratory-confirmed influenza and was associated with high in-hospital mortality. Unadjusted analysis also suggested shorter overall survival in these patients. These findings should be considered exploratory and hypothesis-generating, and require confirmation in larger multicenter studies.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The study examines the incidence and outcomes of acute respiratory deterioration fulfilling acute exacerbation (AE) criteria in hospitalized patients with pre-existing interstitial lung disease (ILD) after laboratory-confirmed seasonal influenza infection, focusing on the clinical burden and prognosis of such events in this vulnerable population.
Evidence base and method
The review is a single-center retrospective cohort study of adults with pre-existing ILD hospitalized with influenza at Toranomon Hospital between April 2014 and April 2019. Influenza was confirmed by rapid diagnostic tests, and AE-criteria fulfillment was adjudicated by two pulmonologists and one radiologist. Baseline characteristics and outcomes were compared using the Mann–Whitney U test, Fisher’s exact test, Kaplan–Meier method, and log-rank test.
Consensus and conclusions
Acute respiratory deterioration fulfilling AE criteria occurred in approximately one fifth (21.3%) of hospitalized patients with ILD after laboratory-confirmed influenza. Patients with AE-criteria events had significantly lower percent predicted forced vital capacity (70.0% vs. 88.0%, p = 0.041) and higher ILD-GAP stage compared to those without such events. The in-hospital mortality rate in patients with AE-criteria events was 30.0%, and overall survival from influenza diagnosis appeared shorter in unadjusted analysis.
Controversies and gaps
The study findings are exploratory and hypothesis-generating, requiring confirmation in larger multicenter studies. The unadjusted survival analysis limits causal inference, and the reliance on rapid diagnostic tests for influenza confirmation may introduce misclassification bias. The absence of multivariable adjustment for confounding factors further constrains the robustness of the observed associations.
Research and practice implications
Future research should prioritize multicenter prospective studies with multivariable adjustment to validate the observed associations and clarify mechanisms linking influenza to AE in ILD. Clinically, heightened surveillance and early intervention strategies for influenza in patients with ILD may be warranted given the high in-hospital mortality and shorter survival observed in those with AE-criteria events.

🚨 突发动态与新闻 / Health News

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

第 6 章:流感 - 疾病控制与预防中心 |疾病预防控制中心 (.gov)

Chapter 6: Influenza - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)
新闻简报The CDC's Chapter 6 on Influenza outlines general guidance for vaccine-preventable disease surveillance, focusing on seasonal influenza viruses. Published on August 5, 2026, the chapter discusses the epidemiology of influenza, including its typical symptoms and incubation period. It highlights that seasonal influenza has historically caused a wide range of impacts, from 9.4 million to 51 million illnesses, with associated medical visits, hospitalizations, and deaths between 2010–11 and 2024–25. The chapter also notes that certain populations, such as older adults, young children, pregnant women, and those with underlying health conditions, are at higher risk for complications. The content does not report a specific outbreak or event but provides a framework for monitoring and controlling influenza. The CDC emphasizes the need for updated vaccines due to antigenic changes in influenza viruses, which can affect vaccine efficacy. The chapter does not specify current investigation status or uncertainty, but it outlines the general approach to influenza surveillance and control.

时间
该章节发布于 2026 年 8 月 5 日 (N5)。内容讨论了 2010-11 至 2024-25 (N15) 的历史流感季节。
地点与对象
内容是一般性的,不指定特定地点或人群。它指的是美国和全球范围内更广泛的 季节性流感 上下文(N15、N35)。
事件
本章提供了有关流感 监测 的一般指导,包括疾病背景、病例定义和 暴发 控制措施。它不报告具体事件,但概述了监测流感(N4、N6)的总体框架。
规模、影响与风险
During 2010–11 through 2024–25, CDC estimated that seasonal influenza caused 9.4 million to 51 million illnesses, 4.4 million to 23 million medical visits, 120,000 to 710,000 hospitalizations, and 6,300 to 52,000 deaths (N15). High-risk groups include persons 65 years of age and older, young children, pregnant women, and those with underlying health conditions (N14).
应对、状态与不确定性
CDC 提供有关 监测、病例调查和 暴发 流感控制的一般指导。它没有具体说明当前的调查状态或不确定性(N4、N6)。

Chapter 6: Influenza - Centers for Disease Control and Prevention | CDC (.gov)

News Brief
The CDC's Chapter 6 on Influenza outlines general guidance for vaccine-preventable disease surveillance, focusing on seasonal influenza viruses. Published on August 5, 2026, the chapter discusses the epidemiology of influenza, including its typical symptoms and incubation period. It highlights that seasonal influenza has historically caused a wide range of impacts, from 9.4 million to 51 million illnesses, with associated medical visits, hospitalizations, and deaths between 2010–11 and 2024–25. The chapter also notes that certain populations, such as older adults, young children, pregnant women, and those with underlying health conditions, are at higher risk for complications. The content does not report a specific outbreak or event but provides a framework for monitoring and controlling influenza. The CDC emphasizes the need for updated vaccines due to antigenic changes in influenza viruses, which can affect vaccine efficacy. The chapter does not specify current investigation status or uncertainty, but it outlines the general approach to influenza surveillance and control.

Manual for the Surveillance of Vaccine-Preventable Diseases Explore This Topic Search Search Clear Search Public Health Table of Contents View all search clear search Manual for the Surveillance of Vaccine-Preventable Diseases Menu clear search Public Health Table of Contents View All Home Chapter 6: Influenza For Public Health Aug. 5, 2026, visit link for details. Key points This chapter provides general guidance for vaccine-preventable disease surveillance, describing the disease background/epidemiology, case investigation and reporting/notification, disease case definitions, and activities for enhancing surveillance, case investigation, and outbreak control for influenza. Disease description Authors: Alicia Budd, MPH; Lisa Grohskopf, MD; C. Todd Davis, PhD; Vivien Dugan, PhD Influenza is an acute respiratory disease caused by infection with influenza viruses. The incubation period ranges from 1 to 4 days.1 Peak virus shedding usually occurs from 1 day before onset of symptoms to 3 days after.123 Typical features of influenza include abrupt onset of fever and respiratory symptoms such as cough (usually nonproductive), sore throat, and coryza, as well as systemic symptoms such as headache, muscle aches, and fatigue.456789 The clinical severity of infection can range from asymptomatic infection to primary viral pneumonia and death. Acute symptoms generally last 2–7 days, although malaise and cough may continue for 2 weeks or longer. Complications of influenza virus infection include secondary bacterial pneumonia, exacerbation of underlying chronic health conditions, and a wide range of additional conditions affecting most organ systems.10 Complications occurring more commonly in children can include otitis media, febrile seizures, encephalopathy, transverse myelitis, myositis, myocarditis, pericarditis, and Reye syndrome.1112131415 Aspirin and other salicylate-containing medications are contraindicated for children and adolescents with influenza-like illness (ILI), as their use during influenza virus infection has been associated with the development of Reye syndrome.16 The sharp rise in influenza-associated acute respiratory illnesses that occurs in the U.S. during annual seasonal epidemics is associated with increased numbers of visits to physicians' offices, walk-in clinics, and emergency departments. Hospitalizations for pneumonia and other complications also increase. Persons 65 years of age and older, young children, pregnant women, and persons of any age with certain underlying health problems are at increased risk for complications of influenza and hospitalization.17 Because influenza seasons are difficult to predict and often fluctuate in length and severity, the overall burden of seasonal influenza varies from year to year. During 2010–11 through 2024–25, CDC estimated that the seasonal impact of influenza ranged from 9.4 million to 51 million illnesses; 4.4 million to 23 million medical visits; 120,000 to 710,000 hospitalizations; and 6,300 to 52,000 deaths.18 Background Influenza viruses can be divided into 4 types: A, B, C, and D. Influenza type C viruses are not usually associated with severe disease and do not cause epidemics, or pandemics. Influenza D viruses primarily affect cattle and are not known to infect or cause illness in people. Neither influenza C nor influenza D viruses will be discussed further here. Influenza type A viruses are divided into subtypes based on surface proteins called hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA).19 There are 16 hemagglutinin and 9 neuraminidase subtypes that circulate in a variety of avian species, and a restricted subgroup of these have infected other animals, such as pigs, horses, cats, ferrets, dogs, and marine mammals (i.e., seals and whales). A few bat species were recently shown to be infected by influenza viruses originally designated as new influenza A subtypes A(H17N10) and A(H18N11).2021 However, these viruses were shown to be incompetent for reassortment with other influenza A viruses.22 Two influenza A virus subtypes have cocirculated in human populations since 1977: influenza A(H1N1) and A(H3N2).23 Reassortment between influenza A(H1N1) and A(H3N2) viruses resulted in the circulation of A(H1N2) viruses during the 2001–02 and 2002–03 influenza seasons.24 In April 2009, a novel influenza A(H1N1) virus, influenza A(H1N1)pdm09—which was different from circulating influenza A(H1N1) viruses—emerged and its subsequent spread resulted in the first influenza pandemic of the 21st century.25 Influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses replaced the prior seasonal influenza A(H1N1) viruses and have been circulating every year since the 2009 pandemic. Influenza B viruses are classified into lineages. Currently, one lineage (B/Victoria) of influenza B viruses is circulating among people. Influenza B/Yamagata lineage viruses have not been identified since March 2020.26 Influenza A and B viruses both undergo gradual, continuous change in the HA and NA proteins, known as antigenic drift. As a result of these antigenic changes, antibodies produced to influenza viruses from an influenza virus infection or influenza vaccination with earlier strains may not be protective against viruses circulating in later years. Consequently, yearly epidemics usually occur in populations, and multiple infections can occur over a person's lifetime. Antigenic changes also necessitate frequent updating of influenza vaccine components to ensure that the vaccine antigens are antigenically similar to those of circulating viruses.2728 In addition to antigenic drift, influenza type A viruses can undergo a more dramatic and abrupt type of antigenic change called an antigenic shift, which occurs when viruses belonging to a new influenza A subtype distinct from currently or recently circulating seasonal influenza A viruses bearing either a novel HA protein or novel HA and NA proteins infect humans.29 A novel HA protein can include a virus of the same subtype but be dramatically antigenically different, as was seen during the 2009 H1N1 influenza pandemic, where the HA likely came from a swine reservoir.25 While antigenic drift occurs continuously, antigenic shift occurs infrequently. When antigenic shift does occur, a large proportion, or even all, of the world's population has no antibody against the new virus. If the novel influenza A virus causes disease and is efficiently transmitted among humans, a worldwide epidemic called a pandemic may result. Novel influenza A viruses, but not influenza B viruses, can cause influenza pandemics. During the 20th century, influenza pandemics occurred with A(H1N1) in 1918, A(H2N2) in 1957, and A(H3N2) in 1968.29 In April 2009, the influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 virus emerged to cause the first influenza pandemic in more than 40 years. The A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses displaced the previously circulating A(H1N1) viruses.30 Vaccination Annual influenza vaccination is recommended to prevent influenza and its complications.31 Information on timing, selection of specific vaccines, administration, and contraindications and precautions for influenza vaccination is available.3132 Influenza vaccination is particularly important for persons who are at potentially increased risk for severe illness and complications from influenza and for influenza-related outpatient, emergency department, or hospital visits (for example, due to age, pregnancy, or presence of certain chronic medical conditions). When influenza vaccine supply is limited, vaccination efforts should focus on vaccination of persons in these groups who do not have contraindications.31 As of September 2025, influenza vaccine types available in the United States include 1) unadjuvanted, egg-based trivalent inactivated influenza vaccines (IIV3s); 2) adjuvanted trivalent egg-based inactivated influenza vaccine (aIIV3); 3) high-dose trivalent egg-based inactivated influenza vaccine (HD-IIV3); 4) trivalent cell culture-based inactivated influenza vaccine (ccIIV3); 5) recombinant trivalent influenza vaccine (RIV3), and 6) live attenuated influenza vaccine (LAIV3). Inactivated and recombinant vaccines are administered by intramuscular injection. LAIV is administered as an intranasal spray.3132 During the 2024-25 and 2025-26 influenza seasons, U.S. influenza vaccines were trivalent, containing hemagglutinin derived from only one influenza B virus from the B/Victoria lineage, because of lack of confirmed detections of influenza B/Yamagata in global surveillance since March 2020.33 Each U.S.-licensed influenza vaccine is approved for a specific age group. Information regarding the age group for which a given vaccine is approved can be found in the package insert.32 Vaccines are available that are approved for persons as young as six months of age. For many vaccine recipients, more than 1 type or brand of vaccine might be appropriate within licensed indications and Advisory Committee on Immunization Practices (ACIP) recommendations. For guidance on selection of vaccine for individual patients, consult the most recent CDC/ACIP influenza vaccination recommendations. One of the best estimates of influenza vaccine efficacy come from randomized controlled trials (RCTs) that compare the rates of laboratory-confirmed influenza or an influenza-related outcome in persons who receive vaccine with those who receive a placebo. However, once a vaccine is recommended for use in a population, for ethical reasons it is difficult to perform randomized trials, because withholding vaccine from these groups could place them at risk for serious complications from influenza. For this reason, ongoing assessment of influenza vaccine effectiveness is generally performed through observational studies. The effectiveness of influenza vaccines varies from season to season and depends on several factors. One factor is the degree of antigenic similarity between the influenza viruses represented in the vaccine and those that circulate during the season. Each year, vaccine strains are selected to represent the strains judged most likely to circulate during the upcoming influenza season in the United States. One or more antigens are typically changed from those in the previous season's vaccine. However, vaccine effectiveness can be lower, or not statistically significant, even in seasons of apparently good antigenic similarity between influenza vaccine components and circulating influenza viruses.34 Moreover, the benefits of influenza vaccination may be of substantially reduced or of no benefit during seasons in which the vaccine strains are antigenically different from the circulating viruses (as was noted, for example, in case-control studies conducted during the 2004–05 and 2014–15 influenza seasons, where the predominant viruses were antigenically and genetically different from the influenza A(H3N2) vaccine virus).353637 Influenza vaccine effectiveness is also affected by the recipient's age, immunocompetence, previous influenza vaccination history, and previous exposure to influenza viruses. In addition, there is some evidence that prior season influenza vaccination may affect current season vaccine effectiveness; however, the impact noted (if any) varies by season and study.3839404142434445 Additionally, estimates of influenza vaccine effectiveness are affected by study characteristics such as the specific outcomes examined (e.g., infection, outpatient-attended illness, or hospitalization) and the manner in which the outcomes are defined (e.g., with laboratory confirmation of infection with tests such as culture or polymerase chain reaction, serology, presence of clinical symptoms, or diagnostic codes). The most accurate influenza vaccine efficacy and effectiveness estimates come from studies that use influenza-specific outcomes, such as laboratory-confirmed (e.g., by culture or reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction [RT-PCR]) influenza virus infection. Such tests can be costly and take time to perform. As more and better diagnostic tests become available, more accurate and consistent assessments of influenza vaccine efficacy and effectiveness may be possible. Among adults, a 2012 systematic review and meta-analysis of influenza vaccine efficacy and effectiveness studies found that efficacy of trivalent inactivated influenza vaccine was reported in 8 of 12 seasons analyzed in 10 randomized controlled trials, with a pooled efficacy against RT-PCR or culture-confirmed influenza of 59% for adults 18 through 65 years of age.46 A 2025 systematic review of randomized and observational studies among children and adults reported a pooled influenza vaccine effectiveness of 48% for adults ages 18 through 64 years and 67% for children against hospitalization.47 Some RCTs have estimated vaccine effectiveness of inactivated influenza vaccine of approximately 50%–70% among adults during some seasons in which the vaccines' influenza A strains were antigenically similar to circulating influenza A viruses.484950 Influenza vaccines are often less effective for older adults than for younger persons. A 2010 review of studies of community-dwelling elderly found that IIV3 was not significantly effective against laboratory-confirmed influenza or ILI.51 A re-analysis of these data using a different stratification method and outcomes measure estimated vaccine effectiveness for laboratory-confirmed influenza of approximately 49%.52 A 2014 systematic review, which pooled data from 35 case-control studies of community-dwelling elderly, found influenza vaccine was effective against laboratory confirmed influenza during periods of regional or widespread influenza activity.53 A case-control study of community-dwelling adults ≥65 years of age found that influenza vaccine during the 2010–11 season was associated with an overall 42% reduction in hospitalizations for laboratory-confirmed influenza, with a higher reduction for influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 (90%) than for influenza A(H3N2) (40%).54 Efforts to improve influenza vaccine effectiveness among adults ≥65 years of age led to the development and licensure of vaccines intended to promote a better immune response in this population, including high-dose and adjuvanted inactivated influenza vaccines. A large randomized comparative efficacy trial of high-dose versus standard-dose trivalent inactivated influenza vaccines among persons ≥65 years of age during the 2011–12 and 2012–13 seasons found 24.2% greater relative efficacy of the high-dose vaccine for protection against laboratory confirmed influenza caused by any viral type or subtype associated with protocol-defined ILI.55 Estimates of influenza vaccine efficacy among children have varied by season and study design. In a randomized controlled trial among children 1–15 years of age, inactivated influenza vaccine efficacy was determined to be 77% against symptomatic culture-positive illness associated with influenza A(H3N2), and 91% against illnesses associated with influenza A(H1N1).56 A randomized, double-blind, placebo-controlled trial conducted during two influenza seasons indicated that, among children 6–24 months of age, inactivated influenza vaccine had 66% efficacy against culture-confirmed influenza illness during the 1999–2000 influenza season, but did not reduce culture-confirmed influenza illness substantially during the 2000–01 influenza season.57 Annual vaccination against influenza is an important method of prevention. Influenza vaccination protects against influenza and its potential complications. During the 2022–23 season, it was estimated that influenza vaccination prevented 5 million influenza illnesses; 2.5 million medical visits; 65,000 influenza hospitalizations; and 5,300 deaths.58 Previous influenza vaccination may offer little or no protection against viruses that have undergone substantial antigenic drift. Even when an influenza vaccine component remains the same, immunity induced by the vaccine declines over time and may not be protective during the next season. Antiviral drugs Antiviral medications with activity against influenza viruses are an important adjunct to influenza vaccine in the control of influenza. Antiviral treatment can reduce the risk of some complications from influenza and is recommended as early as possible for any patient with confirmed or suspected influenza who is hospitalized; has severe, complicated, or progressive illness; or is at higher risk for influenza complications. The benefits of antiviral treatment are likely to be greatest if treatment is started as soon as possible after illness onset; evidence for benefit is strongest in studies in which treatment was started within 48 hours of illness onset.596061626364 Antiviral treatment might still be beneficial in patients with severe, complicated, or progressive illness and in hospitalized patients when administered >48 hours from illness onset.38 Decisions on starting antiviral treatment should not wait for laboratory confirmation of influenza. Six FDA-approved antiviral medications in three drug classes are currently available in the United States: the adamantanes (amantadine and rimantadine), the neuraminidase inhibitors (zanamivir, oseltamivir, and peramivir), and the endonuclease inhibitor baloxavir. However, the adamantanes are not recommended for prevention or treatment of influenza, due to high levels of resistance to these agents among circulating influenza A virus strains.6566 The adamantanes have no activity against influenza B viruses. Oseltamivir, zanamivir, peramivir, and baloxavir are active...

A 高优先级公共卫生新闻

全球呼吸道病毒活动:每周更新 N° 589 - 世界卫生组织 (WHO)

Global Respiratory Virus Activity: Weekly Update N° 589 - World Health Organization (WHO)
新闻简报世界卫生组织每周更新 N° 589(2026 年 8 月 6 日)报告了 2026 年第 30 周的全球呼吸道病毒活动。全球范围内,流感阳性率仍低于 10%,但南半球温带和亚热带地区超过 10%。尽管中美洲、加勒比海地区、热带南美洲以及南亚和东亚单个国家的阳性率超过 10%,但 SARS-CoV-2 活动总体保持较低水平。世卫组织敦促拥有 GISRS 多重试剂盒的国家将流感和 SARS-CoV-2 监测 整合起来,并向区域和全球平台及时提交数据。严重性评估由报告国家、地区和领地提供,监测 数据取自报告 #501 中介绍的前哨和系统病毒学系统。
